    11	fn refuse(s: impl Into<String>) -> RuntimeError {
    12	    RuntimeError::Structural {
    13	        code: "E_ADOPT",
    14	        message: s.into(),
    15	    }
    16	}
    17	fn asset(k: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    18	    match k {
    19	        ColumnType::Optional(k)
    20	        | ColumnType::List { of: k, .. }
    21	        | ColumnType::ListRange { of: k, .. } => asset(k),
    22	        ColumnType::Image { .. } | ColumnType::File { .. } | ColumnType::Video { .. } => true,
    23	        _ => false,
    24	    }
    25	}
    26	// Closed audience inclusion: equality, deny, and Boolean conjunction/union.
    27	// This never infers a business relation between different atomic predicates.
    28	fn audience_subset(next:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,source:&crate::schema::RuleExpr)->bool{
    29	    use crate::schema::RuleExpr::*;
    30	    next==source || *next==Deny || *source==Allow || match (next,source) {
    31	        (Or(xs),_)=>xs.iter().all(|x|audience_subset(x,source)),
    32	        (_,And(xs))=>xs.iter().all(|x|audience_subset(next,x)),
    33	        (And(xs),_)=>xs.iter().any(|x|audience_subset(x,source)),
    34	        (_,Or(xs))=>xs.iter().any(|x|audience_subset(next,x)),
    35	        _=>false,
    36	    }
    37	}
    38	fn mapped_audience(parent:&Table,child:&Table,e:&Extraction,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr)->crate::schema::RuleExpr{
    39	    let mut transported=parent.clone();
    40	    let names=parent.columns.keys().map(|source|{
    41	        let copy=e.fields.iter().find(|(_,old)|*old==source).filter(|(dest,_)|!asset(&child.columns[*dest])&&child.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,crate::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields}if fields.contains(*dest))));
    42	        (source.clone(),copy.map(|(dest,_)|dest.clone()).unwrap_or_else(||format!("$extraction-source:{source}")))
    43	    }).collect();
    44	    transported.column_reads=BTreeMap::from([("$gate".into(),rule.clone())]);
    45	    super::asset_renames::table(&mut transported,&names);
    46	    transported.column_reads.remove("$gate").unwrap()
    47	}
    48	fn probe_context(rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,next:&Schema)->bool{
    49	    use crate::schema::RuleExpr::*;
    50	    match rule {
    51	        Exists{table,index,..}|Created{table,index,..}=>sources.get(table).zip(next.table(table)).is_some_and(|(old,new)|old.index(index)==new.index(index)&&old.rules.read==new.rules.read),
    52	        And(xs)|Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(|r|probe_context(r,sources,next)),Not(r)=>probe_context(r,sources,next),_=>true,
    53	    }
    54	}
    55	fn fields(row: &mut Value) -> Result<&mut crate::value::ObjectFields, RuntimeError> {
    56	    match row {
    57	        Value::Row(f) | Value::Map(f) => Ok(f),
    58	        _ => Err(refuse("invalid extraction row")),
    59	    }
    60	}
    61	fn key(tx:&Tx<'_>,e:&Extraction)->Result<String,RuntimeError>{
    62	    let parent=tx.num(&e.parent)?;let child=tx.num(&e.child)?;
    63	    let aliases:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_asset_column_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    64	    let origin=|t:i64,c:&str|->Result<String,RuntimeError>{
    65	        if !aliases{return Ok(c.into())}
    66	        Ok(tx.conn().query_row("SELECT origin FROM _asset_column_renames WHERE t=?1 AND name=?2",params![t,c],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?.unwrap_or_else(||c.into()))
    67	    };
    68	    let fields=e.fields.iter().map(|(dest,source)|Ok((origin(child,dest)?,origin(parent,source)?))).collect::<Result<BTreeMap<_,_>,RuntimeError>>()?;
    69	    let base=(parent,child,origin(child,&e.reference)?,fields);
    70	    Ok(if e.each {crate::value::digest(&("each-v1",base))}else{crate::value::digest(&base)})
    71	}
    72	fn exists(tx: &Tx<'_>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    73	    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(
    74	        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_extraction_rows')",
    75	        [],
    76	        |r| r.get(0),
    77	    )?)
    78	}
    79	fn load(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
    80	    let bytes: Option<i64> = tx
    81	        .conn()
    82	        .query_row(
    83	            "SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
    84	            params![t, id],
    85	            |r| r.get(0),
    86	        )
    87	        .optional()?;
    88	    let Some(bytes) = bytes else { return Ok(None) };
    89	    Meter::new(Ceilings::job(), Cut::Abort).heap(
    90	        (bytes.max(0) as u64)
    91	            .saturating_mul(64)
    92	            .saturating_add(8192),
    93	    )?;
    94	    let text: String = tx.conn().query_row(
    95	        "SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
    96	        params![t, id],
    97	        |r| r.get(0),
    98	    )?;
    99	    serde_json::from_str(&text)
   100	        .map(Some)
   101	        .map_err(|e| refuse(e.to_string()))
   102	}
   103	fn write(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   104	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(t,id) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",params![t,id,canonical_json(row)])?;
   105	    super::movement::rewrite(tx,t,id,row)?;
   106	    Ok(())
   107	}
   108	fn value(
   109	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   110	    t: i64,
   111	    id: &str,
   112	    row: &Value,
   113	    c: &str,
   114	) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
   115	    if let Ok(v) = row.field(c) {
   116	        return Ok(Some(v.clone()));
   117	    }
   118	    let text: Option<String> = tx
   119	        .conn()
   120	        .query_row(
   121	            "SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
   122	            params![t, id, c],
   123	            |r| r.get(0),
   124	        )
   125	        .optional()?;
   126	    text.map(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| refuse(e.to_string())))
   127	        .transpose()
   128	}
   129	fn transfer(
   130	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   131	    v: &Value,
   132	    from: (&str, &str, &str),
   133	    to: (&str, &str, &str),
   134	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   135	    if let Value::Array(vs) = v {
   136	        for v in vs {
   137	            transfer(tx, v, from, to)?;
   138	        }
   139	        return Ok(());
   140	    }
   141	    if v == &Value::Null {
   142	        return Ok(());
   143	    }
   144	    let id = crate::assets::asset_id(Some(v))
   145	        .ok_or_else(|| refuse("extraction asset has no typed ledger id"))?;
   146	    let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT bytes FROM _assets WHERE id=?1 AND home_table=?2 AND home_id=?3 AND home_column=?4 AND released_at IS NULL",params![id,from.0,from.1,from.2],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   147	    let bytes = bytes.ok_or_else(|| {
   148	        refuse(format!(
   149	            "extraction asset {id} has no live source home; never claim or duplicate it"
   150	        ))
   151	    })?;
   152	    let root = tx
   153	        .meta("activation_asset_root")?
   154	        .ok_or_else(|| refuse("extraction requires activation asset custody"))?;
   155	    if id.is_empty()
   156	        || !id
   157	            .bytes()
   158	            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_')
   159	    {
   160	        return Err(refuse("invalid extraction asset id"));
   161	    }
   162	    let path = std::path::Path::new(&root).join(id);
   163	    let meta = std::fs::symlink_metadata(&path)
   164	        .map_err(|_| refuse(format!("extraction asset {id} bytes are unavailable")))?;
   165	    if !meta.is_file() || meta.len() != bytes as u64 {
   166	        return Err(refuse(format!(
   167	            "extraction asset {id} byte length disagrees with its ledger"
   168	        )));
   169	    }
   170	    if from != to {
   171	    tx.conn().execute(
   172	        "UPDATE _assets SET home_table=?2,home_id=?3,home_column=?4 WHERE id=?1",
   173	        params![id, to.0, to.1, to.2],
   174	    )?;
   175	    }
   176	    Ok(())
   177	}
   178	pub(super) fn transition(
   179	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   180	    next: &Schema,
   181	    previous: Option<&Schema>,
   182	    sources: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
   183	    report: &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport,
   184	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   185	    if next.extractions.is_empty() && previous.is_none_or(|s| s.extractions.is_empty()) {
   186	        return Ok(());
   187	    }
   188	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_rows(definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,child_t INTEGER NOT NULL,child_id TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(definition,parent_t,parent_id),UNIQUE(definition,child_t,child_id)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
   189	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_cells(definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(definition,parent_t,parent_id,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
   190	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_active(child_t INTEGER NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_name TEXT NOT NULL,reference_column TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(child_t,definition)) WITHOUT ROWID; DELETE FROM _extraction_active;")?;
   191	    for e in &next.extractions {
   192	        tx.conn().execute(
   193	            "INSERT INTO _extraction_active VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4)",
   194	            params![tx.num(&e.child)?, key(tx,e)?, e.parent, e.reference],
   195	        )?;
   196	    }
   197	    tx.set_meta("version", super::table_renames::VERSION)?;
   198	    for e in previous
   199	        .into_iter()
   200	        .flat_map(|s| &s.extractions)
   201	        .filter(|e| !next.extractions.contains(e))
   202	    {
   203	        let Some(parent) = next.table(&e.parent) else {
   204	            continue;
   205	        };
   206	        let child = &previous.unwrap().tables[&e.child];
   207	        let pt = tx.num(&e.parent)?;
   208	        let ct = tx.num(&e.child)?;
   209	        let key = key(tx,e)?;
   210	        let mut count = 0;
   211	        let inherited = child
   212	            .through
   213	            .as_ref()
   214	            .is_some_and(|t| t.reference == e.reference && t.parent == e.parent);
   215	        let current_parent = &previous.unwrap().tables[&e.parent];
   216	        if parent.rules.read != crate::schema::RuleExpr::Deny
   217	            && ((inherited && parent.rules.read != current_parent.rules.read)
   218	                || (!inherited && parent.rules.read != child.rules.read))
   219	        {
   220	            return Err(refuse(
   221	                "reverse extraction would broaden the current asset audience; preserve current read policy in an authored bridge",
   222	            ));
   223	        }
   224	        for (dest, source) in &e.fields {
   225	            if asset(&child.columns[dest])
   226	                && parent.columns.contains_key(source)
   227	                && parent.column_reads.get(source) != child.column_reads.get(dest)
   228	            {
   229	                return Err(refuse(
   230	                    "reverse extraction would change the asset column audience; retain its current gate in a bridge",
   231	                ));
   232	            }
   233	        }
   234	        if e.each { each::backward(tx,e,parent,current_parent,child,&key,report)?; continue; }
   235	        super::activation::walk(tx, pt, |id, mut row| {
   236	            let saved:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT child_id FROM _extraction_rows WHERE definition=?1 AND parent_t=?2 AND parent_id=?3",params![key,pt,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   237	            let child_id = if let Some(id) = saved {
   238	                id
   239	            } else {
   240	                let mut found = Vec::new();
   241	                super::activation::walk(tx, ct, |child_id, child| {
   242	                    if child.field(&e.reference) == Ok(&Value::Id(id.into())) {
   243	                        if found.len() < 2 {
   244	                            found.push(child_id.to_owned());
   245	                        }
   246	                    }
   247	                    Ok(())
   248	                })?;
   249	                if found.len() > 1 {
   250	                    return Err(refuse(format!(
   251	                        "extraction {} parent {id} via {}: several child rows compete for {}; run rollback --check --bridge <new-dir>, keep the child population visible, choose a truthful old representation with an authorized mutation, then preview exact rollback",
   252	                        e.child,e.reference,e.fields.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")
   253	                    )));
   254	                }
   255	                let Some(child) = found.pop() else {
   256	                    return Ok(());
   257	                };
   258	                child
   259	            };
   260	            let Some(mut image) = load(tx, ct, &child_id)? else {
   261	                return Err(refuse(format!(
   262	                    "extraction child {}/{child_id} was deleted; old asset has no truthful representation",
   263	                    e.child
   264	                )));
   265	            };
   266	            if image.field(&e.reference) != Ok(&Value::Id(id.into())) {
   267	                return Err(refuse(
   268	                    "extraction child reference changed; refusing to rebind its parent lifetime",
   269	                ));
   270	            }
   271	            let anchored:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND parent_t=?4 AND parent_id=?5 AND invalid=0)",params![ct,child_id,format!("$extract:{key}"),pt,id],|r|r.get(0))?;
   272	            if !anchored {
   273	                return Err(refuse(
   274	                    "extraction child lacks valid parent-lifetime evidence; refusing deletion/id reuse or retrospective recapture",
   275	                ));
   276	            }
   277	            for (destination, source) in &e.fields {
   278	                let kind = &child.columns[destination];
   279	                if !asset(kind) {
   280	                    if row.field(source).ok() != image.field(destination).ok() {
   281	                        return Err(refuse(
   282	                            "extraction immutable scalar copies disagree with the current parent",
   283	                        ));
   284	                    }
   285	                    continue;
   286	                }
   287	                if !parent.columns.contains_key(source) {
   288	                    continue;
   289	                }
   290	                let v = image.field(destination)?.clone();
   291	                crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&e.parent,source,&parent.columns[source],Some(&v))
   292	                    .map_err(|error|refuse(format!("reverse extraction {}.{} → {}.{source}: {error}; keep a widened bridge, repair this child through an authorized mutation, then preview exact rollback",e.child,destination,e.parent)))?;
   293	                retain_cell(tx, &key, pt, id, source, &v)?;
   294	                transfer(
   295	                    tx,
   296	                    &v,
   297	                    (&e.child, &child_id, destination),
   298	                    (&e.parent, id, source),
   299	                )?;
   300	                fields(&mut row)?.insert(source.clone(), v);
   301	                fields(&mut image)?.remove(destination);
   302	                tx.conn().execute(
   303	                    "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
   304	                    params![pt, id, source],
   305	                )?;
   306	            }
   307	            write(tx, pt, id, &row)?;
   308	            write(tx, ct, &child_id, &image)?;
   309	            tx.conn().execute(
   310	                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _extraction_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",
   311	                params![key, pt, id, ct, child_id],
   312	            )?;
   313	            count += 1;
   314	            Ok(())
   315	        })?;
   316	        report.backfills.push(format!(
   317	            "extract {}: moved asset homes back for {count} parent rows",
   318	            e.child
   319	        ));
   320	    }
   321	    for e in next
   322	        .extractions
   323	        .iter()
   324	        .filter(|e| previous.is_none_or(|s| !s.extractions.contains(e)))
   325	    {
   326	        e.admit(next).map_err(refuse)?;
   327	        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#21-wave-27-release-semantics-rel — probes retain the selected source definition's schema, including a newly declared parent.
   328	        let (parent, source_context) = if let Some(parent) = sources.get(&e.parent) {
   329	            (parent, sources)
   330	        } else {
   331	            (&next.tables[&e.parent], &next.tables)
   332	        };
   333	        let child = &next.tables[&e.child];
   334	        let pt = tx.num(&e.parent)?;
   335	        let ct = tx.num(&e.child)?;
   336	        let key = key(tx,e)?;
   337	        let mut count = 0;
   338	        let inherited = child
   339	            .through
   340	            .as_ref()
   341	            .is_some_and(|t| t.reference == e.reference && t.parent == e.parent);
   342	        let source_read = &parent.rules.read;
   343	        if !probe_context(source_read,source_context,next) {return Err(refuse(format!("extraction {} → {}: source read rule `{}` has changed probe/index meaning; retain its typed lookup context in a bridge",e.parent,e.child,source_read.quarry())));}
   344	
   345	        if inherited {
   346	            if !audience_subset(&next.tables[&e.parent].rules.read,source_read) {
   347	                return Err(refuse(
   348	                    "asset extraction cannot broaden the inherited parent read policy; stage an explicit policy change separately",
   349	                ));
   350	            }
   351	        } else if child.rules.read != crate::schema::RuleExpr::Deny {
   352	            if !audience_subset(&child.rules.read,&mapped_audience(parent,child,e,source_read))
   353	            {
   354	                return Err(refuse(
   355	                    "asset extraction requires read through its parent for row-dependent privacy; the destination cannot broaden the source audience",
   356	                ));
   357	            }
   358	        }
   359	        for (dest, source) in &e.fields {
   360	            if let Some(parent_gate)=parent.column_reads.get(source) {
   361	                // Immutable mapped scalar copies are the same authoritative
   362	                // values, so a stricter child audience can name those copies.
   363	                if !probe_context(parent_gate,source_context,next) {return Err(refuse(format!("extraction {}.{source} → {}.{dest}: source payload rule `{}` has changed probe/index meaning; retain its lookup context in a bridge",e.parent,e.child,parent_gate.quarry())));}
   364	                let transported=mapped_audience(parent,child,e,parent_gate);
   365	                let gate=&transported;
   366	                let covered=child.column_reads.get(dest).is_some_and(|g|audience_subset(g,gate))
   367	                    || (inherited&&audience_subset(&next.tables[&e.parent].rules.read,parent_gate))
   368	                    || (!inherited&&audience_subset(&child.rules.read,gate));
   369	                if !covered {return Err(refuse(format!("extraction {}.{source} → {}.{dest}: preserve source read rule `{}` on the child column or through its parent; an authored stricter audience is allowed",e.parent,e.child,parent_gate.quarry())));}
   370	            }
   371	        }
   372	        let populated: bool =
   373	            tx.conn()
   374	                .query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1)", [pt], |r| {
   375	                    r.get(0)
   376	                })?;
   377	        let consumed_each=e.each && !parent.columns.contains_key(each::source(e,child));
   378	        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — definition custody survives an empty parent population; every resumed child still needs its original anchor.
   379	        let resume_each=consumed_each && each::known(tx,&key)?;
   380	        if consumed_each && !resume_each {
   381	            let child_history:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1)",[ct],|r|r.get(0))?;
   382	            if populated || child_history {return Err(refuse("extract each source list is absent and no successfully applied extraction owns this suspended definition; preserve independent child history in an authored bridge"));}
   383	        }
   384	        for (dest, source) in e.fields.iter().filter(|_| populated) {
   385	            if resume_each && asset(&child.columns[dest]) {continue;}
   386	            let kind = parent.columns.get(source).ok_or_else(|| {
   387	                refuse(format!("extraction source {}.{source} is absent", e.parent))
   388	            })?;
   389	            let same = if e.each && asset(&child.columns[dest]) {each::item_type(kind)==Some(&child.columns[dest])} else {kind == &child.columns[dest] || matches!(&child.columns[dest],ColumnType::Optional(inner) if inner.as_ref()==kind)};
   390	            if !same
   391	                || parent.engine_field(source)
   392	                || parent.maintained.contains_key(source)
   393	                || parent.written_by.contains_key(source)
   394	            {
   395	                return Err(refuse(
   396	                    format!("extraction {}.{source} → {}.{dest} requires identical authoritative types or required → optional widening",e.parent,e.child),
   397	                ));
   398	            }
   399	            if asset(kind) && next.tables[&e.parent].columns.contains_key(source) {
   400	                return Err(refuse(
   401	                    "extraction must remove the source asset column; one asset cannot have two active homes",
   402	                ));
   403	            }
   404	            if !asset(kind)&&(!parent.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,crate::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(source)))||matches!(kind,ColumnType::Ref{..}|ColumnType::Optional(_))){return Err(refuse("extraction scalar copies require immutable source fields without reference/captured authority"))}
   405	        }
   406	        if e.each { if resume_each {each::resume(tx,e,parent,child,&key,report)?;}else{each::forward(tx,e,parent,child,&key,report)?;} continue; }
   407	        super::activation::walk(tx, pt, |id, mut row| {
   408	            let saved:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT child_id FROM _extraction_rows WHERE definition=?1 AND parent_t=?2 AND parent_id=?3",params![key,pt,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   409	            let child_id = saved.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| id.to_owned());
   410	            let present = load(tx, ct, &child_id)?;
   411	            let independent:bool=if saved.is_none(){tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _extraction_rows WHERE child_t=?1 AND child_id=?2)",params![ct,child_id],|r|r.get(0))?}else{false};
   412	            if saved.is_none() && (present.is_some()||independent) {
   413	                return Err(refuse(format!(
   414	                    "extraction destination {}/{child_id} already has independent history",
   415	                    e.child
   416	                )));
   417	            }
   418	            if saved.is_some(){
   419	                let anchored:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND parent_t=?4 AND parent_id=?5 AND invalid=0)",params![ct,child_id,format!("$extract:{key}"),pt,id],|r|r.get(0))?;
   420	                if present.is_none()||!anchored{return Err(refuse("returning extraction lacks its receipt-owned child or valid parent-lifetime evidence; never recreate pruned/deleted history"))}
   421	            }
   422	            let mut image = present.unwrap_or_else(|| {
   423	                Value::Row(
   424	                    BTreeMap::from([
   425	                        ("id".into(), Value::Id(child_id.clone())),
   426	                        (e.reference.clone(), Value::Id(id.into())),
   427	                    ])
   428	                    .into(),
   429	                )
   430	            });
   431	            if image.field(&e.reference) != Ok(&Value::Id(id.into())) {
   432	                return Err(refuse("extraction cannot rebind a child parent"));
   433	            }
   434	            for (destination, source) in &e.fields {
   435	                let kind = &child.columns[destination];
   436	                let Some(v) = value(tx, pt, id, &row, source)? else {
   437	                    continue;
   438	                };
   439	                if asset(kind) {
   440	                    if image.field(destination).is_ok_and(|old| old != &v && !(saved.is_some() && old == &Value::Null)) {
   441	                        return Err(refuse(
   442	                            "extraction destination asset conflicts with its source",
   443	                        ));
   444	                    }
   445	                    retain_cell(tx, &key, pt, id, source, &v)?;
   446	                    transfer(
   447	                        tx,
   448	                        &v,
   449	                        (&e.parent, id, source),
   450	                        (&e.child, &child_id, destination),
   451	                    )?;
   452	                    fields(&mut image)?.insert(destination.clone(), v);
   453	                    fields(&mut row)?.remove(source);
   454	                    tx.conn().execute(
   455	                        "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
   456	                        params![pt, id, source],
   457	                    )?;
   458	                } else if saved.is_none() {
   459	                    fields(&mut image)?.insert(destination.clone(), v);
   460	                } else if image.field(destination).ok() != Some(&v) {
   461	                    return Err(refuse(
   462	                        "extraction immutable scalar copies disagree with the returning parent",
   463	                    ));
   464	                }
   465	            }
   466	            crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, child);
   467	            write(tx, pt, id, &row)?;
   468	            write(tx, ct, &child_id, &image)?;
   469	            tx.conn().execute(
   470	                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _extraction_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",
   471	                params![key, pt, id, ct, child_id],
   472	            )?;
   473	            super::dormant::capture_refs(
   474	                tx,
   475	                ct,
   476	                &child_id,
   477	                &format!("$extract:{key}"),
   478	                &ColumnType::Ref {
   479	                    table: e.parent.clone(),
   480	                },
   481	                &Value::Id(id.into()),
   482	            )?;
   483	            count += 1;
   484	            Ok(())
   485	        })?;
   486	        report.backfills.push(format!(
   487	            "extract {}: prepared {count} child rows and moved existing asset homes",
   488	            e.child
   489	        ));
   490	    }
   491	    tx.set_meta("activation_population", "true")?;
   492	    Ok(())
   493	}
   494	pub(super) fn delete(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   495	    if exists(tx)? {
   496	        each::delete(tx,t,id)?;
   497	        tx.conn().execute(
   498	            "DELETE FROM _extraction_cells WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2",
   499	            params![t, id],
   500	        )?;
   501	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _extraction_rows WHERE (parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2) OR (child_t=?1 AND child_id=?2)",params![t,id])?;
   502	    }
   503	    Ok(())
   504	}
   505	
   506	pub(super) fn observe_write(
   507	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   508	    t: i64,
   509	    id: &str,
   510	    row: &Value,
   511	    old: Option<&Value>,
   512	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   513	    if !exists(tx)? {
   514	        return Ok(());
   515	    }
   516	    let active:Vec<(String,String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition,parent_name,reference_column FROM _extraction_active WHERE child_t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   517	    for (key, parent, column) in active {
   518	        let binding = format!("$extract:{key}");
   519	        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — only birth or a real reference change can capture a parent lifetime; an asset edit cannot repair missing provenance.
   520	        if old.is_some_and(|old| old.field(&column).ok() == row.field(&column).ok()) {
   521	            continue;
   522	        }
   523	        if let Ok(value) = row.field(&column) {
   524	            super::dormant::capture_refs(
   525	                tx,
   526	                t,
   527	                id,
   528	                &binding,
   529	                &ColumnType::Ref { table: parent },
   530	                value,
