pub(super) fn test_payload(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    value: &Value,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    check(tx, "r", t, id, None, value)
}

pub(super) fn snapshot_restore(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO temp._activation_images SELECT 'r',t,id,'',data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 ON CONFLICT(relation,t,id,col) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",[t])?;
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn verify_expected(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    relation: &str,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    assert!(matches!(
        relation,
        "r" | "_dormant_rows" | "_dormant_narrow_rows"
    ));
    let same = equal_json("d.data", "e.data");
    let n=tx.conn().query_row(&format!("SELECT count(*) FROM {relation} d JOIN temp._activation_images e ON e.t=d.t AND e.id=d.id WHERE e.relation=?1 AND e.t=?2 AND e.id=?3 AND e.col='' AND ({same})"),params![relation,t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;
    expected(n, 1, relation)
}

/// Existing retained rows may be replaced by a newly projected live population.
/// Verify that every old source still has a live copy before clearing it.
pub(super) fn retained_coverage(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let missing:i64=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM _dormant_rows s WHERE t=?1 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r d WHERE d.t=s.t AND d.id=s.id)",[t],|r|r.get(0))?;
    expected(missing, 0, "r")
}

pub(super) fn population(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    source: &str,
    destination: &str,
    t: i64,
    copied: i64,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    expected(rows(tx, destination, t)?, copied, destination)?;
    assert!(matches!(source, "r" | "_dormant_rows"));
    assert!(matches!(destination, "r" | "_dormant_rows"));
    let sql = format!(
        "SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM {source} WHERE t=?1) - (SELECT count(*) FROM {source} s JOIN {destination} d ON d.t=s.t AND d.id=s.id WHERE s.t=?1)"
    );
    let missing = tx.conn().query_row(&sql, [t], |r| r.get::<_, i64>(0))?;
    expected(missing, 0, destination)?;
    let sql = format!(
        "SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM {destination} WHERE t=?1) - (SELECT count(*) FROM {source} WHERE t=?1)"
    );
    let extra = tx.conn().query_row(&sql, [t], |r| r.get::<_, i64>(0))?;
    expected(extra, 0, destination)?;
    let mut stmt = tx
        .conn()
        .prepare(&format!("SELECT id FROM {source} WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id"))?;
    let mut ids = stmt.query([t])?;
    while let Some(row) = ids.next()? {
        let id: String = row.get(0)?;
        verify_expected(tx, destination, t, &id)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn expected(actual: i64, expected: i64, relation: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if actual != expected {
        return Err(RuntimeError::Store(format!(
            "SNAPBACK_ACTIVATION_COPY_MISMATCH: {relation} destination verification expected {expected}, observed {actual}; source retained"
        )));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
pub(super) mod fault {
    use super::*;
    thread_local! { static ERASE: std::cell::RefCell<Option<(String,bool)>> = const {std::cell::RefCell::new(None)}; }
    pub fn arm(relation: &str, corrupt: bool) {
        ERASE.with(|f| *f.borrow_mut() = Some((relation.into(), corrupt)));
    }
    pub fn run(
        tx: &Tx<'_>,
        relation: &str,
        t: i64,
        id: &str,
        _: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let action = ERASE.with(|f| {
            let mut f = f.borrow_mut();
            if f.as_ref().is_some_and(|(r, _)| r == relation) {
                f.take().map(|(_, corrupt)| corrupt)
            } else {
                None
            }
        });
        if let Some(corrupt) = action {
            let sql = if corrupt {
                format!("UPDATE {relation} SET data='{{}}' WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")
            } else {
                format!("DELETE FROM {relation} WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")
            };
            tx.conn().execute(&sql, params![t, id])?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    thread_local! {static LATE:std::cell::Cell<Option<bool>>=const{std::cell::Cell::new(None)};}
    pub fn arm_late() {
        LATE.with(|f| f.set(Some(false)));
    }
    pub fn arm_late_payload() {
        LATE.with(|f| f.set(Some(true)));
    }
    pub fn late(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if let Some(payload) = LATE.with(|f| f.replace(None)) {
            tx.conn().execute(
                if payload {
                    "UPDATE r SET data=json_set(data,'$.Row.n.Int',8) WHERE t=?1"
                } else {
                    "DELETE FROM r WHERE t=?1"
                },
                [t],
            )?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

// Expected counts are captured before restoration markers can execute any code.
// Recheck immediately before deleting the retained source, not just after INSERT.
pub(super) fn rows(tx: &Tx<'_>, relation: &str, t: i64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    assert!(matches!(relation, "r" | "_dormant_rows"));
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(
        &format!("SELECT count(*) FROM {relation} WHERE t=?1"),
        [t],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}
pub(super) fn cells(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, column: &str) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM _dormant_values v JOIN r ON r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id WHERE v.t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=v.t AND n.id=v.id)",params![t,column],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0))?)
}
pub(super) fn verify_cells(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    column: &str,
    copied: i64,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    #[cfg(test)]
    if copied > 0 {
        fault::late(tx, t)?;
    }
    expected(cells(tx, t, column)?, copied, "r cell restoration")?;
    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT v.id FROM _dormant_values v JOIN r ON r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id WHERE v.t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=v.t AND n.id=v.id)")?;
    let mut ids = stmt.query(params![t, column])?;
    while let Some(row) = ids.next()? {
        let id: String = row.get(0)?;
        verify_expected(tx, "r", t, &id)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn verify_narrowed(tx: &Tx<'_>, copied: i64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let actual = tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT count(*) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN r ON r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id",
        [],
        |r| r.get::<_, i64>(0),
    )?;
    #[cfg(test)]
    if copied > 0 {
        let t: i64 = tx.conn().query_row(
            "SELECT n.t FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN r ON r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id LIMIT 1",
            [],
            |r| r.get(0),
        )?;
        fault::late(tx, t)?;
    }
    expected(actual, copied, "r narrowed restoration")?;
    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT n.t,n.id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN r ON r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id ORDER BY n.t,n.id")?;
    let mut ids = stmt.query([])?;
    while let Some(row) = ids.next()? {
        let t: i64 = row.get(0)?;
        let id: String = row.get(1)?;
        verify_expected(tx, "r", t, &id)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}
//! One-child historical extraction moves the existing asset home atomically.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — deterministic identities, no upload or second home.
use super::*;
#[path = "extraction_each.rs"]
mod each;
use crate::{
    meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter},
    schema::{ColumnType, Extraction},
};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
fn refuse(s: impl Into<String>) -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural {
        code: "E_ADOPT",
        message: s.into(),
    }
}
fn asset(k: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    match k {
        ColumnType::Optional(k)
        | ColumnType::List { of: k, .. }
        | ColumnType::ListRange { of: k, .. } => asset(k),
        ColumnType::Image { .. } | ColumnType::File { .. } | ColumnType::Video { .. } => true,
        _ => false,
    }
}
// Closed audience inclusion: equality, deny, and Boolean conjunction/union.
// This never infers a business relation between different atomic predicates.
fn audience_subset(next:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,source:&crate::schema::RuleExpr)->bool{
    use crate::schema::RuleExpr::*;
    next==source || *next==Deny || *source==Allow || match (next,source) {
        (Or(xs),_)=>xs.iter().all(|x|audience_subset(x,source)),
        (_,And(xs))=>xs.iter().all(|x|audience_subset(next,x)),
        (And(xs),_)=>xs.iter().any(|x|audience_subset(x,source)),
        (_,Or(xs))=>xs.iter().any(|x|audience_subset(next,x)),
        _=>false,
    }
}
fn mapped_audience(parent:&Table,child:&Table,e:&Extraction,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr)->crate::schema::RuleExpr{
    let mut transported=parent.clone();
    let names=parent.columns.keys().map(|source|{
        let copy=e.fields.iter().find(|(_,old)|*old==source).filter(|(dest,_)|!asset(&child.columns[*dest])&&child.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,crate::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields}if fields.contains(*dest))));
        (source.clone(),copy.map(|(dest,_)|dest.clone()).unwrap_or_else(||format!("$extraction-source:{source}")))
    }).collect();
    transported.column_reads=BTreeMap::from([("$gate".into(),rule.clone())]);
    super::asset_renames::table(&mut transported,&names);
    transported.column_reads.remove("$gate").unwrap()
}
fn probe_context(rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,next:&Schema)->bool{
    use crate::schema::RuleExpr::*;
    match rule {
        Exists{table,index,..}|Created{table,index,..}=>sources.get(table).zip(next.table(table)).is_some_and(|(old,new)|old.index(index)==new.index(index)&&old.rules.read==new.rules.read),
        And(xs)|Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(|r|probe_context(r,sources,next)),Not(r)=>probe_context(r,sources,next),_=>true,
    }
}
fn fields(row: &mut Value) -> Result<&mut crate::value::ObjectFields, RuntimeError> {
    match row {
        Value::Row(f) | Value::Map(f) => Ok(f),
        _ => Err(refuse("invalid extraction row")),
    }
}
fn key(tx:&Tx<'_>,e:&Extraction)->Result<String,RuntimeError>{
    let parent=tx.num(&e.parent)?;let child=tx.num(&e.child)?;
    let aliases:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_asset_column_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    let origin=|t:i64,c:&str|->Result<String,RuntimeError>{
        if !aliases{return Ok(c.into())}
        Ok(tx.conn().query_row("SELECT origin FROM _asset_column_renames WHERE t=?1 AND name=?2",params![t,c],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?.unwrap_or_else(||c.into()))
    };
    let fields=e.fields.iter().map(|(dest,source)|Ok((origin(child,dest)?,origin(parent,source)?))).collect::<Result<BTreeMap<_,_>,RuntimeError>>()?;
    let base=(parent,child,origin(child,&e.reference)?,fields);
    Ok(if e.each {crate::value::digest(&("each-v1",base))}else{crate::value::digest(&base)})
}
fn exists(tx: &Tx<'_>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_extraction_rows')",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}
fn load(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
    let bytes: Option<i64> = tx
        .conn()
        .query_row(
            "SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
            params![t, id],
            |r| r.get(0),
        )
        .optional()?;
    let Some(bytes) = bytes else { return Ok(None) };
    Meter::new(Ceilings::job(), Cut::Abort).heap(
        (bytes.max(0) as u64)
            .saturating_mul(64)
            .saturating_add(8192),
    )?;
    let text: String = tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?;
    serde_json::from_str(&text)
        .map(Some)
        .map_err(|e| refuse(e.to_string()))
}
fn write(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(t,id) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",params![t,id,canonical_json(row)])?;
    super::movement::rewrite(tx,t,id,row)?;
    Ok(())
}
fn value(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    row: &Value,
    c: &str,
) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
    if let Ok(v) = row.field(c) {
        return Ok(Some(v.clone()));
    }
    let text: Option<String> = tx
        .conn()
        .query_row(
            "SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
            params![t, id, c],
            |r| r.get(0),
        )
        .optional()?;
    text.map(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| refuse(e.to_string())))
        .transpose()
}
fn transfer(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    v: &Value,
    from: (&str, &str, &str),
    to: (&str, &str, &str),
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if let Value::Array(vs) = v {
        for v in vs {
            transfer(tx, v, from, to)?;
        }
        return Ok(());
    }
    if v == &Value::Null {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let id = crate::assets::asset_id(Some(v))
        .ok_or_else(|| refuse("extraction asset has no typed ledger id"))?;
    let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT bytes FROM _assets WHERE id=?1 AND home_table=?2 AND home_id=?3 AND home_column=?4 AND released_at IS NULL",params![id,from.0,from.1,from.2],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
    let bytes = bytes.ok_or_else(|| {
        refuse(format!(
            "extraction asset {id} has no live source home; never claim or duplicate it"
        ))
    })?;
    let root = tx
        .meta("activation_asset_root")?
        .ok_or_else(|| refuse("extraction requires activation asset custody"))?;
    if id.is_empty()
        || !id
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_')
    {
        return Err(refuse("invalid extraction asset id"));
    }
    let path = std::path::Path::new(&root).join(id);
    let meta = std::fs::symlink_metadata(&path)
        .map_err(|_| refuse(format!("extraction asset {id} bytes are unavailable")))?;
    if !meta.is_file() || meta.len() != bytes as u64 {
        return Err(refuse(format!(
            "extraction asset {id} byte length disagrees with its ledger"
        )));
    }
    if from != to {
    tx.conn().execute(
        "UPDATE _assets SET home_table=?2,home_id=?3,home_column=?4 WHERE id=?1",
        params![id, to.0, to.1, to.2],
    )?;
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn transition(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    sources: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    report: &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if next.extractions.is_empty() && previous.is_none_or(|s| s.extractions.is_empty()) {
        return Ok(());
    }
    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_rows(definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,child_t INTEGER NOT NULL,child_id TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(definition,parent_t,parent_id),UNIQUE(definition,child_t,child_id)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_cells(definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(definition,parent_t,parent_id,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_active(child_t INTEGER NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_name TEXT NOT NULL,reference_column TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(child_t,definition)) WITHOUT ROWID; DELETE FROM _extraction_active;")?;
    for e in &next.extractions {
        tx.conn().execute(
            "INSERT INTO _extraction_active VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4)",
            params![tx.num(&e.child)?, key(tx,e)?, e.parent, e.reference],
        )?;
    }
    tx.set_meta("version", super::table_renames::VERSION)?;
    for e in previous
        .into_iter()
        .flat_map(|s| &s.extractions)
        .filter(|e| !next.extractions.contains(e))
    {
        let Some(parent) = next.table(&e.parent) else {
            continue;
        };
        let child = &previous.unwrap().tables[&e.child];
        let pt = tx.num(&e.parent)?;
        let ct = tx.num(&e.child)?;
        let key = key(tx,e)?;
        let mut count = 0;
            }
            source.columns.insert(column.clone(),old.kind);
            if let Some(default)=old.default {source.defaults.insert(column.clone(),default);}
            if !old.authoritative {
                if let Some(owner)=candidate.maintained.get(column) {source.maintained.insert(column.clone(),owner.clone());}
                if let Some(capture)=candidate.recorded.get(column) {source.recorded.insert(column.clone(),capture.clone());}
            }
        }
    }
    let restored_images=!restoring.is_empty() || cells.iter().any(|(n,c,_)|next.table(n).is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(c)));
    // Current authority cells precede destination narrowing: historical
    // authored copies may be outside a newly narrowed type even though the
    // current parent value fits it. Newly restored narrowed rows reconcile
    // individually inside transition as well.
    crate::engine::through_activation::reconcile_manual(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
    if backfilling {
        super::narrowed::restore_images(tx,next,previous,now,&source_definitions,&authority_sources,true)?;
        super::backfills::retire_snapshot(tx,&source_definitions,previous,now)?;
    }else{super::narrowed::retire_sources(tx,next,previous,now)?;}
    // Reverse projections still read the complete predecessor snapshot,
    // including tables absent from the rollback target.
    super::extractions::transition(tx,next,previous,&source_definitions,report)?;
    super::backfills::transition(tx,next,previous,&source_definitions,now,report)?;
    // Archive removed tables only after source expiry and every migration
    // projection have closed over the predecessor image.
    if backfilling {if let Some(previous)=previous {for (name,old) in &previous.tables {
        if next.tables.contains_key(name) || old.ephemeral.is_some() || previous.maintain_for(name).is_some(){continue;}
        let t=tx.num(name)?;
        super::movement::retained_coverage(tx,t)?;
        let mut copied=0i64;
        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,mut row|{
            let native=super::backfills::native_absences(tx,t,id,&row)?;
            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,old);
            super::backfills::preserve_native_absence(&mut row,&native);
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(t,id) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",params![t,id,canonical_json(&row)])?;
            super::movement::image(tx,"_dormant_rows",t,id,None,&row)?;
            for (column,kind) in &old.columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column) {
                if !super::authority_origin::live_input(old,column) {capture_refs(tx,t,id,column,kind,value)?;}
                capture_through_parent(tx,name,old,id,column,&row)?;
            }}copied+=1;Ok(())
        })?;
        super::movement::population(tx,"r","_dormant_rows",t,copied)?;
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=?1",[t])?;
        for index in old.indexes.keys(){tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?])?;}
    }}}
    super::narrowed::transition(tx,next,previous,now,restored_images,&source_definitions,&authority_sources)?;
    crate::engine::through_activation::reconcile_manual(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
    check_references(tx, next,&authority_sources)?;
    // Restored rows have no predecessor indexes. Install the identity scan
    // before the ordinary read-through barrier; preserve historical captures.
    let identity_tables:BTreeSet<String>=if tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true") {
        next.tables.iter().filter(|(n,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(n).is_none()).map(|(n,_)|n.clone()).collect()
    } else {restoring.iter().map(|(n,_)|n.clone()).collect()};
    for name in &identity_tables {
        let table = &next.tables[name];
        let t = tx.num(name)?;
        let index = tx.num(&format!("{name}.byId"))?;
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[index])?;
        super::activation::walk(tx, t, |id, row| {
            for key in index_keys(&table.indexes["byId"], &row, id) {
                tx.conn().execute(
                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
                    params![index, key, id],
                )?;
            }
            Ok(())
        })?;
    }
    for (name, table) in next.tables.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
        let t = tx.num(name)?;
        for column in table.columns.keys() {
            let expected_cells=super::movement::cells(tx,t,column)?;
            if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
                tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'column',v.t,v.column_name,v.id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND v.column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=v.t AND n.id=v.id)",params![t,column])?;
            } else {
                let ids=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT v.id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND v.column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=v.t AND n.id=v.id)")?.query_map(params![t,column],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
                if !ids.is_empty() {tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_column_restored_ids:{name}.{column}"),&serde_json::to_string(&ids).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;}
            }
            super::movement::verify_cells(tx,t,column,expected_cells)?;
            tx.conn().execute(
                "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_values.t AND n.id=_dormant_values.id)",
                params![t, column],
            )?;
            tx.conn().execute(
                "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_refs.t AND n.id=_dormant_refs.id)",
                params![t, column],
            )?;
            tx.conn().execute(
                "DELETE FROM _dormant_columns WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?3 AND column_name=?2)",
                params![name, column,t],
            )?;

   364	                // A non-null retained predecessor value is an exact preimage.
   365	                // Legacy rollback loops could manufacture optional null cells;
   366	                // the explicit down declaration is the author's substitution

   371	            }
   372	            if projected_outputs.len()<target_outputs.len(){preimages+=1;if preimage_ids.len()<5{preimage_ids.push(id.to_owned());}}

   417	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#191-declared-old-views-and-renames — all down expressions observe the assembled newer snapshot before any forward preimage is restored.

   634	                let others:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT e.definition FROM _backfill_evidence e JOIN _backfill_definitions d ON d.digest=e.definition WHERE e.t=?1 AND e.id=?2")?.query_map(params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   635	                for other in others {if let Some(e)=evidence(tx,t,id,&other)? {
   636	                    for c in fill.outputs.intersection(&e.after.keys().cloned().collect()) {
   637	                        if e.active || current.field(c).ok()!=e.before.get(c) {conflict=true;}
   638	                    }
   639	                }}
   640	                if conflict {
   641	                    bad.add(id,"native output also has a different transformation's history; resolve its intended view in a bridge");return Ok(())
   642	                }
   643	                let complete=fill.outputs.iter().all(|c|current.field(c).is_ok() && validate_output(&fill.table,table,c,current.field(c).unwrap()).is_ok());
   644	                let fingerprint=crate::value::digest(&native_image);
   645	                if !complete {bad.add(id,"native backfill output image is missing or was explicitly pruned; use a bridge or restore a backup");return Ok(())}
   646	                if !native.contains(&fingerprint) {
   647	                    let args=native_inputs(tx,t,id,&current,fill.inputs.keys().map(String::as_str),row_source.as_deref())?;
   648	                    if fill.inputs.iter().any(|(c,k)|crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&fill.table,c,k,args.get(c)).is_err()) {
   649	                        bad.add(id,"native backfill inputs changed outside its owning release and cannot be recomputed; use a bridge");return Ok(())
   650	                    }
   651	                    let mut context=migration_context(tx,now)?;context.args=args;
   652	                    let mut runner=Runner::new(tx,&source,context,Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort),0);
   653	                    let agrees=match runner.run(&fill.program) {Ok(Value::Row(p)|Value::Map(p)) if runner.read_cut().is_none()=>p.iter().all(|(c,v)|current.field(c).ok()==Some(v)) && p.keys().cloned().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()==fill.outputs,_=>false};
   654	                    if !agrees {bad.add(id,"native backfill inputs changed outside its owning release and recomputation differs from its authored output; preserve it in a bridge");return Ok(())}
   655	                }
   656	                return Ok(())
   657	            }
   658	            let mut original_absence=prior.as_ref().map_or_else(||physically_absent.intersection(&fill.inputs.keys().cloned().collect()).cloned().collect(),|e|e.absent.clone());
   659	            for c in fill.inputs.keys() {if (row.field(c).is_err() || row.field(c)==Ok(&Value::Null)) && tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_unprovided WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,c],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {original_absence.insert(c.clone());}}
   660	            // Resolve retained inputs before source defaults can synthesize null
   661	            // for a hidden optional column with a provided archived cell.
   662	            let retained=native_inputs(tx,t,id,&row,fill.inputs.keys().map(String::as_str),row_source.as_deref())?;
   663	            if let Some(table)=row_source.as_deref(){materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,table);}
   664	            let mut args=BTreeMap::new();
   665	            for (c,kind) in &fill.inputs {
   666	                let value=retained.get(c).cloned();
   667	                if crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&fill.table,c,kind,value.as_ref()).is_err() {
   668	                    bad.add(id,&format!("historical input {}.{c} is absent or does not fit {kind}; use a typed bridge to repair it",fill.table));return Ok(())
   669	                }
   670	                if let Some(prior)=&prior {if prior.inputs.field(c).ok()==value.as_ref() {
   671	                    let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,input_ref(&key,c)],|r|r.get(0))?;
   672	                    if invalid {bad.add(id,&format!("historical backfill reference {c} lost its parent lifetime"));return Ok(())}
   673	                }}
   674	                args.insert(c.clone(),value.unwrap_or(Value::Null));
   675	            }
   676	            let inputs=Value::Row(args.clone().into());
   677	            let mut context=migration_context(tx,now)?;context.args=args;
   678	            let mut runner=Runner::new(tx,&source,context,Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort),0);
   679	            let patch=match runner.run(&fill.program) {Ok(Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f))=>f.into_iter().collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(),Ok(_)=>{bad.add(id,"backfill did not return an object");return Ok(())},Err(e)=>{bad.add(id,&e.to_string());return Ok(())}};
   680	            if runner.read_cut().is_some() {bad.add(id,"historical lookup did not complete; reduce per-row lookup work before adoption");return Ok(())}
   681	            if patch.keys().cloned().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()!=fill.outputs {return Err(refusal("backfill returned undeclared output fields"))}
   682	            let fields=current.as_object()?;let mut before=BTreeMap::new();let mut desired=patch.clone();
   683	            let mut preimage_meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);
   684	            for (c,value) in &patch {
   685	                // A new transform may consume an older receipt's accepted view,
   686	                // but cannot overwrite a distinct retained authored native value.
   687	                let others:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND active=0 AND definition!=?3 AND retained_outputs!='[]'")?.query_map(params![t,id,key],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   688	                for other in others {if let Some(retained)=evidence(tx,t,id,&other)? {
   689	                    if retained.retained.contains(c)&&retained.authored.contains(c)&&retained.after.get(c)!=Some(value)
   690	                        &&retained.reversed.get(c).is_some_and(|view|view.as_ref()==current.field(c).ok()) {
   691	                        bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} retained authored output {} conflicts with new backfill {} computation {}; select a bridge that exposes the retained native value, repair it through an authorized mutation, then preview the new named transform",fill.table,c,display_cell(retained.after.get(c)),fill.name,display_cell(Some(value))));return Ok(())
   692	                    }
   693	                }}
   694	                if let Err(e)=validate_output(&fill.table,table,c,value) {bad.add(id,&e.to_string());return Ok(())}
   695	                if let Some(kind)=row_source.as_deref().and_then(|t|t.columns.get(c)) {if protected(kind) && prior.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.before.contains_key(c)) {bad.add(id,&format!("cannot overwrite historical reference or asset {}.{c}; derive into a new column",fill.table));return Ok(())}}
   696	                if prior.is_none() && fill.name.starts_with("$rename:up:") {
   697	                    if let Some(existing)=physical_value(tx,t,id,&current,c,&mut preimage_meter)? {
   698	                        if &existing!=value {bad.add(id,&format!("rename destination {}.{c} already has a conflicting value: destination {}, source {}; resolve through an authorized mutation under a bridge; see guide deploy",fill.table,display_cell(Some(&existing)),display_cell(Some(value))));return Ok(())}
   699	                    }
   700	                }
   701	                let transformed=prior.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.active || e.retained.contains(c));
   702	                if transformed {
   703	                    if let Some(value)=prior.as_ref().and_then(|e|e.before.get(c)){before.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}
   704	                } else if row_source.as_deref().is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(c)) {
   705	                    if let Some(value)=source_value(tx,t,id,&current,c,row_source.as_deref(),&mut preimage_meter)? {before.insert(c.clone(),value);}
   706	                }
   707	                if let Some(prior)=&prior {
   708	                    let deps_same=prior.inputs==inputs && prior.after==patch;
   709	                    let current_field=fields.get(c);
   710	                    let original=prior.before.get(c);
   711	                    let expected=prior.reversed.get(c).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||original.cloned());
   712	                    let actual=if transformed {None}else{source_value_impl(tx,t,id,&current,c,row_source.as_deref(),&mut preimage_meter,false)?};
   713	                    if !transformed && actual.as_ref()!=expected.as_ref() && actual.as_ref()!=prior.after.get(c) && actual.as_ref()!=Some(value) {
   714	                        bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} changed while backfill {} was absent: older-release value {}, expected old view {}, retained newer value {}, recomputed value {}; keep a bridge and resolve through an authorized mutation; see guide deploy",fill.table,c,fill.name,display_cell(actual.as_ref()),display_cell(expected.as_ref()),display_cell(prior.after.get(c)),display_cell(Some(value))));return Ok(())
   715	                    }
   716	                    let computed=if prior.computed.is_empty(){&prior.after}else{&prior.computed};
   717	                    if prior.authored.contains(c)&&(prior.inputs!=inputs||computed!=&patch)&&current_field!=Some(value){
   718	                        bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} authored output {} conflicts with changed historical inputs/dependencies and recomputation {}; retain a bridge and resolve explicitly",fill.table,c,display_cell(current_field),display_cell(Some(value))));return Ok(())
   719	                    }
   720	                    if prior.authored.contains(c) && computed==&patch && prior.inputs==inputs && current_field==prior.after.get(c) && current_field.is_some() {
   721	                        desired.insert(c.clone(),current_field.unwrap().clone());
   722	                    } else if deps_same && transformed && current_field.is_some() {
   723	                        // Added columns retain edits under v1's dormant storage.
   724	                        desired.insert(c.clone(),current_field.unwrap().clone());
   725	                    } else if transformed && !deps_same && current_field!=prior.after.get(c) && current_field!=original && current_field!=Some(value) {
   726	                        bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} was edited and its historical inputs changed: current {}, retained output {}, recomputed {}; keep a bridge and resolve through an authorized mutation; see guide deploy",fill.table,c,display_cell(current_field),display_cell(prior.after.get(c)),display_cell(Some(value))));return Ok(())
   727	                    }
   728	                }
   729	            }
   730	            for(c,value)in &desired {if let Err(e)=validate_output(&fill.table,table,c,value){bad.add(id,&e.to_string());return Ok(())}}
   731	            let mut after=patch.clone();
   732	            for c in prior.as_ref().into_iter().flat_map(|e|&e.authored){if let Some(value)=desired.get(c){after.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}}
   733	            let e=Evidence{computed:if prior.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|!e.authored.is_empty()){patch.clone()}else{BTreeMap::new()},authored:prior.as_ref().map(|e|e.authored.clone()).unwrap_or_default(),reversed:BTreeMap::new(),retained:BTreeSet::new(),absent:original_absence,epoch:epoch.unwrap_or(fresh_epoch),active:true,inputs,before,after};
   734	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO temp._backfill_pending VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",params![t,id,key,canonical_json(&desired),canonical_json(&e)])?;count+=1;
   735	            Ok(())
   736	        })?;
   737	        if bad.count>0{return Err(refusal(format!("backfill {} {}: {}",fill.table,fill.name,bad.describe())))}
   738	        if count>0{report.backfills.push(format!("{} {} transformed {count} {}",fill.table,fill.name,if count==1 {"row"}else{"rows"}));}
   739	    }
   740	    }
   741	    restore_snapshot(tx)?;
   742	    // Stream patches; avoid retaining the whole transformed population in RAM.
   743	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition,patch,evidence,octet_length(patch)+octet_length(evidence) FROM temp._backfill_pending ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   744	    let mut rows=stmt.query([])?;
   745	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   746	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;
   747	        let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap((r.get::<_,i64>(5)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192)).map_err(|err|refusal(format!("backfill staged reversal evidence exceeds its read budget: {err}; reduce the per-row migration image")))?;
   748	        let patch:BTreeMap<String,Value>=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(3)?)?;let e:Evidence=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(4)?)?;
   749	        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
   750	        patch_retained(tx,t,&id,patch.iter().map(|(c,v)|(c.as_str(),Some(v))))?;
   751	        if let Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=&mut row {f.extend(patch);}
   752	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;super::movement::rewrite(tx,t,&id,&row)?;
   753	        let prior=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?;
   754	        save(tx,t,&id,&key,&e,true)?;
   755	        for c in e.after.keys() {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition='' AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,c])?;}
   756	        let fill=next.backfills.iter().find(|f|f.key()==key).ok_or_else(||refusal("missing historical declaration"))?;
   757	        let row_source=row_source(tx,t,&id,sources.get(&fill.table))?;
   758	        for(c,kind)in &fill.inputs {
   759	            // A generic id argument cannot erase the source column's lifetime.
   760	            let kind=row_source.as_deref().and_then(|t|t.columns.get(c)).filter(|k|protected(k)).unwrap_or(kind);
   761	            if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c) {
   762	                let binding=input_ref(&key,c);
   763	                let unchanged=prior.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|p.inputs.field(c).ok()==Some(value));
   764	                capture_input_refs(tx,t,&id,c,&binding,kind,value,unchanged)?;
   765	            }
   766	        }
   767	    }
   768	    drop(rows);drop(stmt);
   769	    tx.conn().execute_batch("DROP TABLE temp._backfill_pending; DROP TABLE temp._backfill_originals;")?;
   770	    // The source-index rebuild and every transformed dependency require full
   825	        let mut statement=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition FROM _backfill_definitions WHERE digest IN (SELECT definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 OR retained_outputs!='[]' UNION SELECT definition FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE active=1 OR retained_outputs!='[]')")?;
   826	        for text in statement.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {
   827	            let f:Backfill=decode(&text?)?;out.entry(f.table).or_default().extend(f.outputs);
   828	        }
   829	        let mut statement=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition FROM _downfill_definitions WHERE digest IN (SELECT definition FROM _backfill_evidence UNION SELECT definition FROM _backfill_pruned)")?;
   830	        for text in statement.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {
   831	            let f:Backfill=decode(&text?)?;out.entry(f.table).or_default().extend(f.outputs);
   832	        }
   833	    }
   834	    Ok(out)
   835	}
   836	fn validate_lookup(tx:&Tx<'_>,ti:i64,prefix:usize,table:&str,index:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   837	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT k,id FROM i WHERE ti=?1 ORDER BY k")?;let mut rows=stmt.query([ti])?;
   838	    let mut prior=None;let mut first=String::new();let mut duplicate=false;let mut bad=super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
   839	    while let Some(row)=rows.next()? {
   840	        let key:Vec<u8>=row.get(0)?;let id:String=row.get(1)?;let mut group=crate::key::decode(&key).ok_or_else(||refusal("invalid historical lookup key"))?;group.truncate(prefix);
   841	        if group.contains(&Value::Null){continue;}
   842	        if prior.as_ref()==Some(&group) {
   843	            if !duplicate {bad.add(&first,"duplicate historical lookup key");duplicate=true;}
   844	            bad.add(&id,"duplicate historical lookup key");
   845	        }else{prior=Some(group);first=id;duplicate=false;}
   846	    }
   847	    if bad.count>0{return Err(refusal(format!("historical lookup {table}.{index} is not unique: {}; prepare a unique bridge before deriving values",bad.describe())))}
   848	    Ok(())
   849	}
   850	
   851	// Each original adoption cohort saw one snapshot. Re-entry restores that same
   852	// view across all its declarations; later cohorts read earlier cohorts' outputs.
   853	fn restore_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   854	    tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=(SELECT o.data FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id)",[])?;Ok(())
   855	}
   856	// A reversed, originally absent cell must have the same meaning in explicit
   857	// arguments, point lookups and lookup indexes. Null is a temporary source-view
   858	// marker; the saved physical image is restored before committing any patch.
   859	fn mask_unprovided(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   860	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#191-declared-old-views-and-renames — close each source cursor before mutating its physical image.
   861	    let mut after=(i64::MIN,String::new());
   862	    loop {
   863	        let next:Option<(i64,String,i64)>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT r.t,r.id,octet_length(r.data) FROM r WHERE (r.t,r.id)>(?1,?2) AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_unprovided m WHERE m.t=r.t AND m.id=r.id) ORDER BY r.t,r.id LIMIT 1",params![after.0,after.1],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?))).optional()?;
   864	        let Some((t,id,bytes))=next else{break};
   865	        after=(t,id.clone());
   866	        let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);meter.heap((bytes.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   867	        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
   868	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   869	        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {
   870	            let mut columns=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM temp._backfill_unprovided WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?;
   871	            for c in columns.query_map(params![t,id],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {fields.entry(c?).or_insert(Value::Null);}
   872	        }
   873	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   874	    }Ok(())
   875	}
   876	// Recorded later cohorts originally saw earlier releases' completed patches.
   877	// Replaying those older cohorts virtually preserves that ordering; fresh
   878	// declarations still run first against the untouched predecessor snapshot.
   879	fn replay_earlier_cohorts(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   880	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,octet_length(patch),patch FROM temp._backfill_pending WHERE CAST(json_extract(evidence,'$.epoch') AS INTEGER)<?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   881	    let mut rows=stmt.query([epoch])?;
   882	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   883	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;
   884	        let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   885	        let Some(bytes)=bytes else{continue};
   886	        let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap((bytes.max(0) as u64).saturating_add(r.get::<_,i64>(2)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   887	        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
   888	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   889	        let patch:BTreeMap<String,Value>=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(3)?)?;
   890	        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {fields.extend(patch);}
   891	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   892	    }Ok(())
   893	}
   894	
   895	fn historical_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64,keys:&BTreeSet<String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   896	    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 AND epoch=?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   897	    let mut rows=q.query([epoch])?;
   898	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   899	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;if !keys.contains(&key){continue;}
   900	        let data:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   901	        let Some(data)=data else {continue};
   902	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   903	        let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;let e=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?.unwrap();
   904	        let fields=match &mut row {Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=>f,_=>return Err(refusal("invalid historical row"))};
   905	        if let Value::Row(inputs)|Value::Map(inputs)=&e.inputs {for(c,value)in inputs {
   906	            if e.absent.contains(c) || !fields.contains_key(c) || fields.get(c)==Some(value) {
   907	                let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,input_ref(&key,c)],|r|r.get(0))?;
   908	                if invalid {return Err(refusal(format!("historical backfill reference {c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")));}
   909	                if e.absent.contains(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}else{fields.entry(c.clone()).or_insert(value.clone());}
   910	            }
   911	        }}
   912	        for c in e.after.keys() {if !e.before.contains_key(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),Value::Null);}}
   913	        for(c,value)in e.before {fields.insert(c,value);}
   914	        // Keep the receipt's omitted-input meaning separate from its later
   915	        // rollback output preimage/default.
   916	        for c in &e.absent {if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c){fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}}
   917	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   918	    } Ok(())
   919	}
   920	fn rebuild_reads(tx:&Tx<'_>,source:&Schema,reads:&BTreeSet<(String,String)>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   921	    for (name,index) in reads {
   922	        let old=source.table(name).and_then(|t|t.index(index)).ok_or_else(||refusal(format!("migration lookup {name}.{index} is not declared in its source schema")))?;let ti=tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?;
   923	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[ti])?;
   924	        super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|id,mut row|{
   925	            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&source.tables[name]);
   926	            for key in index_keys(old,&row,id){tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![ti,key,id])?;}Ok(())
   927	        })?;
   928	        if old.unique {validate_lookup(tx,ti,old.components.len(),name,index)?;}
   929	    } Ok(())
   930	}
   931	
   932	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#6-bakeoff-repairs — computed values obey the ordinary text minimum/format law before narrowing.
   933	fn validate_output(name:&str,table:&Table,column:&str,value:&Value)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   934	    crate::engine::validate::validate_column(name,column,&table.columns[column],Some(value))?;
   935	    for constraint in &table.constraints {if let crate::schema::Constraint::Text{field,min,format}=constraint {
   936	        if field==column {if let Value::String(text)=value {crate::engine::validate::validate_text(&format!("{name}.{column}"),text,*min,*format)?;}}
   937	    }}Ok(())
   938	}
   939	
   940	// Ordinary cell bindings can carry a newer transformed reference type than
   941	// the archived whole-row definition. Copy those exact lifetime bindings before
   942	// restoration consumes its ordinary ledger; same-value replay never rebinds.
   943	fn capture_input_refs(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str,binding:&str,kind:&crate::schema::ColumnType,value:&Value,unchanged:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   944	    if !unchanged {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,binding])?;}
   945	    let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap(value.retained_heap_bytes().saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   946	    fn ids(value:&Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value {Value::Id(id)=>{out.insert(id.clone());},Value::Array(values)=>for value in values {ids(value,out)},_=>()}}
   947	    let mut values=BTreeSet::new();ids(value,&mut values);let mut known=BTreeSet::new();
   948	    if unchanged {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND parent_id NOT IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?4))",params![t,id,binding,canonical_json(&values)])?;}
   949	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT parent_t,parent_id,invalid FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3")?;
   950	    let mut rows=stmt.query(params![t,id,column])?;
   951	    while let Some(row)=rows.next()? {
   952	        let parent:String=row.get(1)?;if !values.contains(&parent){continue;}
   953	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6)",params![t,id,binding,row.get::<_,i64>(0)?,parent,row.get::<_,i64>(2)?])?;known.insert(parent);
   954	    }
   955	    super::dormant::capture_refs_preserving(tx,t,id,binding,kind,value,&known)
   956	}
   957	
   958	fn input_ref(key:&str,column:&str)->String {format!("$backfill:{key}:{column}")}
   959	
   960	// A prior transformation's retained added output is not its own old argument
   961	// on forward selection after rollback. Other declarations still see the new
   962	// predecessor snapshot rather than an old cohort's overwritten columns.
   963	fn inactive_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,_previous:Option<&Schema>,keys:&BTreeSet<String>,epoch:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   964	    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=0 AND epoch=?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   965	    let mut rows=q.query([epoch])?;
   966	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   967	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;if !keys.contains(&key){continue;}
   968	        let e=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?.unwrap();
   969	        if e.retained.is_empty() && e.absent.is_empty(){continue;}
   970	        let data:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;let Some(data)=data else{continue};
   971	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   972	        let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {
   973	            for c in &e.retained {fields.insert(c.clone(),e.before.get(c).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null));}
   974	            for c in &e.absent {
   975	                let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,input_ref(&key,c)],|r|r.get(0))?;
   976	                if invalid {return Err(refusal(format!("historical backfill reference {c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")));}
   977	                if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c){fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}
   978	            }
   979	        }
   980	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   981	    }Ok(())
   982	}

   984	// Empty definition denotes currently omitted physical input cells; a definition
   985	// digest denotes absence at that receipt's adoption. Defaults may materialize
   986	// during retention without turning omitted inputs into authored values.
   987	pub(crate) fn prepare_inputs(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,previous:Option<&Schema>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   988	    if next.backfills.is_empty() && next.downfills.is_empty()
   989	        && previous.is_none_or(|s|s.backfills.is_empty() && s.downfills.is_empty()) && !present(tx)? {return Ok(());}
   990	    enable(tx)?;
