//! Hiding cannot turn an absent safety witness into a grant.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — current clauses also need their safety population.
use super::*;
use crate::{
    ir::{BinaryOp, Builtin, Expr, Program, Stmt},
    schema::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm},
};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
fn refuse() -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_ADOPT",message:"intentional rollback hiding can weaken an authority/guard through a changed lookup population; retain the safety witness in a visible bridge".into()}
}
fn reference(t: &RuleTerm, hidden: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
    t.references().any(|(table, _)| hidden.contains(table))
}
fn nonnull(t: &RuleTerm) -> bool {
    matches!(t, RuleTerm::Viewer) || matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(v) if v!=&Value::Null)
}
fn null(t: &RuleTerm) -> bool {
    matches!(t, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null))
}
fn rule(r: &RuleExpr, hidden: &BTreeSet<String>, negative: bool) -> bool {
    use RuleExpr::*;
    match r {
        Not(r) => rule(r, hidden, !negative),
        And(rs) | Or(rs) => rs.iter().any(|r| rule(r, hidden, negative)),
        Exists {
            table,
            key,
            equalities,
            ..
        }
        | Created {
            table,
            key,
            equalities,
            ..
        } => {
            negative && hidden.contains(table)
                || key
                    .iter()
                    .chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_, v)| v))
                    .any(|v| reference(v, hidden))
        }
        Eq(a, b) | Ne(a, b) => {
            let eq = matches!(r, Eq(..)) ^ negative;
            [(a, b), (b, a)]
                .iter()
                .any(|(a, b)| reference(a, hidden) && if eq { !nonnull(b) } else { !null(b) })
        }
        Compare(_, a, b) => reference(a, hidden) || reference(b, hidden),
        _ => false,
    }
}
fn json_taint(v: &serde_json::Value, env: &BTreeSet<String>, hidden: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
    match v {
        serde_json::Value::Object(o) => {
            if o.get("Variable")
                .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                .is_some_and(|n| env.contains(n))
            {
                return true;
            }
            if o.get("table")
                .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                .is_some_and(|n| hidden.contains(n))
            {
                return true;
            }
            o.values().any(|v| json_taint(v, env, hidden))
        }
        serde_json::Value::Array(a) => a.iter().any(|v| json_taint(v, env, hidden)),
        _ => false,
    }
}
fn signature(v: &impl serde::Serialize, points: &BTreeSet<String>) -> String {
    fn expand(v: &mut serde_json::Value, points: &BTreeSet<String>, depth: usize) {
        if depth > 64 {
            return;
        }
        if let Some(name) = v.get("Variable").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
            if let Some(value) = points
                .iter()
                .find_map(|p| p.strip_prefix(&format!("alias:{name}:")))
            {
                *v = serde_json::from_str(value).unwrap();
                expand(v, points, depth + 1);
                return;
            }
        }
        match v {
            serde_json::Value::Object(m) => {
                for v in m.values_mut() {
                    expand(v, points, depth + 1)
                }
            }
            serde_json::Value::Array(a) => {
                for v in a {
                    expand(v, points, depth + 1)
                }
            }
            _ => (),
        }
    }
    let mut v = serde_json::to_value(v).unwrap();
    expand(&mut v, points, 0);
    canonical_json(&v)
}
fn point_key(e: &Expr, points: &BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
    match e {
        Expr::Get(r) => Some(format!("{}:{}", r.table, signature(&r.key, points))),
        Expr::Builtin {
            builtin: Builtin::RequireUncapped,
            args,
        } if args.len() == 1 => point_key(&args[0], points),
        _ => None,
    }
}
fn guarded(table: &str, key: &Expr, points: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
    points.contains(&format!("guard:{table}:{}", signature(&vec![key], points)))
}
fn point(e: &Expr, env: &BTreeSet<String>, hidden: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
    match e {
        Expr::Get(r) => {
            hidden.contains(&r.table)
                && !json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(&r.key).unwrap(), env, hidden)
        }
        Expr::Builtin {
            builtin: Builtin::RequireUncapped,
            args,
        } => args.len() == 1 && point(&args[0], env, hidden),
        _ => false,
    }
}
fn expr(e: &Expr, env: &BTreeSet<String>, hidden: &BTreeSet<String>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(match e {
        Expr::Literal(_) => false,
        Expr::Variable(n) => env.contains(n),
        Expr::Block { body, value } => {
            let mut local = env.clone();
            body_check(body, &mut local, &mut BTreeSet::new(), hidden)?;
            expr(value, &local, hidden)?
        }
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => expr(left, env, hidden)? | expr(right, env, hidden)?,
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Field { base: value, .. } => expr(value, env, hidden)?,
        Expr::Array(a) => {
            let mut result = false;
            for e in a {
                result |= expr(e, env, hidden)?;
            }
            result
        }
        Expr::Object(m) => {
            let mut result = false;
            for e in m.values() {
                result |= expr(e, env, hidden)?;
            }
            result
        }
        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => {
            let mut result = expr(base, env, hidden)?;
            for e in fields.values() {
                result |= expr(e, env, hidden)?;
            }
            result
        }
        Expr::Scan(scan) => {
            let mut encoded = serde_json::to_value(scan).unwrap();
            encoded.as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("table");
            if json_taint(&encoded, env, hidden) {
                return Err(refuse());
            }
            hidden.contains(&scan.table)
        }
        Expr::Merge(m) => {
            if json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(m).unwrap(), env, hidden) {
                return Err(refuse());
            }
            false
        }
        Expr::Builtin { builtin, args } => {
            if matches!(builtin, Builtin::JobQuery { .. })
                && json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(builtin).unwrap(), env, hidden)
            {
                return Err(refuse());
            }
            let mut result = false;
            for e in args {
                result |= expr(e, env, hidden)?;
            }
            if matches!(builtin, Builtin::JobAs) && result {
                return Err(refuse());
            }
            result
        }
        _ => json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(e).unwrap(), env, hidden),
    })
}
fn body_check(
    body: &[Stmt],
    env: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
    points: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
    hidden: &BTreeSet<String>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    for s in body {
        match s {
            Stmt::Let { name, value }
            | Stmt::Const { name, value }
            | Stmt::Assign { name, value } => {
                let p = point(value, env, hidden);
                let taint = expr(value, env, hidden)?;
                points.retain(|p| !p.starts_with(&format!("{name}=>")));
                if p {
                    points.insert(name.clone());
                    if let Some(key) = point_key(value, points) {
                        points.insert(format!("{name}=>{key}"));
                    }
                } else {
                    points.remove(name);
                }
                if taint {
                    env.insert(name.clone());
                } else {
                    env.remove(name);
                }
                let alias = if matches!(
                    value,
                    Expr::Variable(_)
                        | Expr::Literal(_)
                        | Expr::Input(
                            crate::ir::Input::Viewer
                                | crate::ir::Input::Now
                                | crate::ir::Input::ReadTime
                        )
                ) {
                    signature(value, points)
                } else {
                    canonical_json(&Expr::Variable(format!("$opaque:{s:p}:{name}")))
                };
                points.retain(|p| !p.starts_with(&format!("alias:{name}:")));
                points.insert(format!("alias:{name}:{alias}"));
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => {
                if !expr(condition, env, hidden)? {
                    continue;
                }
                if let Expr::Binary {
                    op: BinaryOp::Ne,
                    left,
                    right,
                } = condition
                {
                    let name = match (left.as_ref(), right.as_ref()) {
                        (Expr::Variable(n), Expr::Literal(Value::Null))
                        | (Expr::Literal(Value::Null), Expr::Variable(n)) => Some(n),
                        _ => None,
                    };
                    if let Some(n) = name.filter(|n| points.contains(*n)) {
                        if let Some(key) = points
                            .iter()
                            .find_map(|v| v.strip_prefix(&format!("{n}=>")).map(str::to_owned))
                        {
                            points.insert(format!("guard:{key}"));
                        }
                        env.remove(n);
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                if let Expr::Exists(r) = condition {
                    if hidden.contains(&r.table)
                        && !json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(&r.key).unwrap(), env, hidden)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                return Err(refuse());
            }
            Stmt::If {
                condition,
                then_body,
                else_body,
            } => {
                if expr(condition, env, hidden)? {
                    return Err(refuse());
                }
                let mut a = env.clone();
                let mut b = env.clone();
                body_check(then_body, &mut a, &mut points.clone(), hidden)?;
                body_check(else_body, &mut b, &mut points.clone(), hidden)?;
                env.extend(a);
                env.extend(b);
                points.clear();
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                if json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(domain).unwrap(), env, hidden) {
                    return Err(refuse());
                }
                body_check(body, env, points, hidden)?;
            }
            Stmt::Return(e) => {
                expr(e, env, hidden)?;
            }
            Stmt::Push { target, value } => {
                if expr(value, env, hidden)? {
                    env.insert(target.clone());
                    points.remove(target);
                }
            }
            Stmt::Insert { row, .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } => {
                if expr(row, env, hidden)? {
                    return Err(refuse());
                }
            }
            Stmt::Update { table, key, patch }
            | Stmt::Upsert {
                table,
                key,
                row: patch,
            } => {
                if hidden.contains(table) && !guarded(table, key, points) {
                    return Err(refuse());
                }
                if expr(key, env, hidden)? || expr(patch, env, hidden)? {
                    return Err(refuse());
                }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { table, key } => {
                if hidden.contains(table) && !guarded(table, key, points) || expr(key, env, hidden)?
                {
                    return Err(refuse());
                }
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget {
                target,
                patch,
                condition,
                result,
                ..
            } => {
                if hidden.contains(&target.table)
                    && !points.contains(&format!(
                        "guard:{}:{}",
                        target.table,
                        signature(&target.key, points)
                    ))
                {
                    return Err(refuse());
                }
                if json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(&target.key).unwrap(), env, hidden)
                    || expr(patch, env, hidden)?
                    || json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(condition).unwrap(), env, hidden)
                {
                    return Err(refuse());
                }
                if let Some(name) = result {
                    env.remove(name);
                    points.remove(name);
                }
            }
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                if hidden.contains(&target.table)
                    && !points.contains(&format!(
                        "guard:{}:{}",
                        target.table,
                        signature(&target.key, points)
                    ))
                    || json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(&target.key).unwrap(), env, hidden)
                {
                    return Err(refuse());
                }
            }
            _ => {
                if json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(s).unwrap(), env, hidden) {
                    return Err(refuse());
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn validate(
    schema: &Schema,
    programs: &BTreeMap<String, Program>,
    hidden: &BTreeSet<String>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if hidden.is_empty() {
        return Ok(());
    }
    // A positive audience witness can disappear without physically archiving
    // the row it protects. Follow that visibility dependency as well.
    let mut closure = hidden.clone();
    loop {
        let before = closure.len();
        for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
            if json_taint(
                &serde_json::to_value(&table.rules.read).unwrap(),
                &BTreeSet::new(),
                &closure,
            ) {
                closure.insert(name.clone());
            }
        }
        if closure.len() == before {
            break;
        }
    }
    let hidden = &closure;
    // A positive freeze witness has the opposite safety polarity to a grant:
    // disappearing locks can enable a write, including a trusted system write.
    if schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.frozen_columns.values().any(|r|json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(r).unwrap(),&BTreeSet::new(),hidden))) {return Err(refuse());}

    // Point-presence discharge is valid only when surviving payload cells do
    // not change visibility. No payload-policy implication is guessed here.
    if schema.tables.values().any(|t| {
        t.column_reads
            .values()
            .any(|r| json_taint(&serde_json::to_value(r).unwrap(), &BTreeSet::new(), hidden))
    }) {
        return Err(refuse());
    }
    if schema.maintains.iter().any(|m| hidden.contains(&m.source))
        || schema.tables.values().any(|t| {
            t.maintained
                .values()
                .any(|s| hidden.contains(s.split('.').next().unwrap_or(s)))
        })
    {
        return Err(refuse());
    }
    for table in schema.tables.values() {
        for r in [
            &table.rules.read,
            &table.rules.insert,
            &table.rules.update,
            &table.rules.delete,
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .chain(table.column_reads.values())
        {
            if rule(r, hidden, false) {
                return Err(refuse());
            }
        }
    }
    for p in programs.values() {
        body_check(&p.body, &mut BTreeSet::new(), &mut BTreeSet::new(), hidden)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}
//! Row authority and dormant cell provenance are separate facts.
// @ref LLP 3000.014.000#implementation-proof-clarifications — validate the immediate manual policy before using older live evidence.
use super::*;
use crate::schema::through::{PARENT_DEADLINE, DEADLINE};

pub(super) fn mode(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str)->Result<Option<u8>,RuntimeError> {
    if !live_activation::active(tx)? {return Ok(None);}
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row("SELECT CASE kind WHEN 'source-manual' THEN 0 WHEN 'source-captured' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END FROM temp._live_restored WHERE kind IN ('source-manual','source-captured','source-live') AND t=?1 AND col='' AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?)
}
pub(super) fn record(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,old:&Table)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    if !live_activation::active(tx)? {return Ok(());}
    let kind=match &old.through {None=>"source-manual",Some(e) if e.live.is_some()=>"source-live",Some(_)=>"source-captured"};
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored VALUES(?1,?2,'',?3)",params![kind,t,id])?;
    if let Some(edge)=old.through.as_ref().filter(|e|e.live.is_none()) {
        for col in edge.fields.keys().map(String::as_str).chain([PARENT_DEADLINE,DEADLINE]) {
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored VALUES('source-capture',?1,?2,?3)",params![t,col,id])?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn live_cell(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,col:&str)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND live_source=1)",params![t,id,col],|r|r.get(0))?)
}
/// Captured-only restoration preserves its old audience. A manual row with
/// live-origin inputs must prove its *current* audience before recapture.
pub(super) fn validate(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,name:&str,old:&Table,row:&Value,now:i64,narrowed:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    let Some(table)=next.table(name) else {return Ok(())};
    let t=tx.num(name)?;let id=row.field("id")?.as_str()?;
    if needs_proof(tx,t,id,old,table,narrowed)? {
        crate::engine::through_activation::forward_authority(tx,next,sources,name,old,row,now,true)?;
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored VALUES('live-authority',?1,'',?2)",params![t,id])?;
    }
    Ok(())
}
/// Never let unused live cells replace a captured row's own audience/deadline.
/// Missing live-origin inputs remain absent until the capture barrier fills
/// just those new inputs from the current parent.
pub(super) fn overlay(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,old:Option<&Table>,table:&Table,row:&mut Value,values:Vec<(String,String)>)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    let captured=old.and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).filter(|e|e.live.is_none());
    for (col,data) in values {
        if !table.columns.contains_key(&col) {continue;}
        let protected=captured.is_some_and(|e|e.fields.contains_key(&col) || matches!(col.as_str(),PARENT_DEADLINE|DEADLINE));
        if protected {continue;}
        let stale=captured.is_some() && live_activation::active(tx)? && live_cell(tx,t,id,&col)?;
        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=row {
            if stale {fields.remove(&col);} else {fields.insert(col,serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?);}
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn live_input(table:&Table,column:&str)->bool {
    table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some() && (e.fields.contains_key(column) || matches!(column,PARENT_DEADLINE|DEADLINE)))
}
/// Current policy inputs are refreshable; the immutable parent lifetime and
/// authored references remain bound even when old policy targets disappear.
pub(super) fn obsolete_ref(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str,table:&Table,old:Option<&Table>)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    let Some(edge)=&table.through else {return Ok(false)};
    if column==PARENT_DEADLINE || !(edge.fields.contains_key(column) || column==DEADLINE) {return Ok(false);}
    let protected=if mode(tx,t,id)?.is_some() {
        tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._live_restored WHERE kind='source-capture' AND t=?1 AND id=?2 AND col=?3)",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
    } else {old.and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_none() && (e.fields.contains_key(column) || column==DEADLINE))};
    if protected {return Ok(false);}
    Ok(edge.live.is_some() || live_activation::active(tx)? && live_cell(tx,t,id,column)?)
}

/// Reserve declared parent scratch only for callbacks that can fetch it.
/// Captured-only restoration keeps its existing actual-image admission.
pub(super) fn forward_fetch(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,old:Option<&Table>,table:&Table)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    match old {Some(old)=>needs_proof(tx,t,id,old,table,false),None=>Ok(false)}
}
/// One predicate for actual parent fetches and their preflight reservation.
fn needs_proof(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,old:&Table,table:&Table,narrowed:bool)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    if !live_activation::active(tx)? || old.through.is_some() {return Ok(false);}
    let Some(edge)=&table.through else {return Ok(false)};
    if edge.live.is_some() {return Ok(true);}
    for col in edge.fields.keys().map(String::as_str).chain([PARENT_DEADLINE,DEADLINE]) {
        if live_cell(tx,t,id,col)? {return Ok(true);}
    }
    if !narrowed {return Ok(false);}
    let history:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND live_source=0)",params![t,id,PARENT_DEADLINE],|r|r.get(0))?;
    Ok(!history)
}
pub(super) fn live_row(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,old:&Table,table:&Table)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    if !live_activation::active(tx)? {return Ok(false);}
    if old.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) || table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) {return Ok(true);}
    for col in table.columns.keys() {if live_cell(tx,t,id,col)? {return Ok(true);}}
    needs_proof(tx,t,id,old,table,true)
}
//! Release row lifetimes whose present values cannot inhabit the selected type.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#91-per-row-narrowed-value-dormancy — hide a reference-closed population; never coerce values.
use super::*;
use crate::schema::{ColumnType, Constraint};

pub(super) fn present(conn: &Connection) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_dormant_narrow_rows')",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}

fn incompatible(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema, name: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    incompatible_except(tx,schema,name,row,&[])
}
fn incompatible_except(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema,name:&str,row:&Value,authority:&[String])->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    let table = &schema.tables[name];
    for (column,kind) in &table.columns {
        if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) || authority.contains(column) {continue;}
        if let Ok(value)=row.field(column) {
            if incompatible_column(tx, table, name, column, kind, value)? { return Ok(true); }
        }
    }
    Ok(false)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — the warning and activation share the value predicate, including ledger-backed assets.
pub(super) fn incompatible_column(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &Table, name: &str, column: &str, kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    if crate::engine::validate::validate_column(name, column, kind, Some(value)).is_err() { return Ok(true); }
    // @ref LLP 3000.038#1-the-type — asset facts belong to the ledger, including optional/list leaves.
    match super::activation::validate_assets(tx, kind, Some(value)) {
        Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE" | "E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", .. }) => return Ok(true),
        Err(error) => return Err(error),
        Ok(()) => (),
    }
    Ok(table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { field, min, format }
        if field == column && matches!(value, Value::String(s)
            if crate::engine::validate::validate_text(field, s, *min, *format).is_err()))))
}

fn migratable(kind:&ColumnType)->bool {
    match kind {ColumnType::Optional(k)=>migratable(k),ColumnType::Image{..}|ColumnType::Video{..}|ColumnType::File{..}|ColumnType::List{..}|ColumnType::ListRange{..}|ColumnType::Ref{..}=>false,_=>true}
}

fn refs(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), v) => refs(inner, v, out),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(vs)) => {
            for v in vs {
                refs(of, v, out);
            }
        }
        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
        _ => {}
    }
}

pub(super) fn transition(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    now: i64,
    restored_images: bool,
    source_definitions: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    authority_sources: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    // Backfill sources already retired through the ordinary action closure,
    // before patching. Never re-test predecessor deadlines on transformed rows.
    if next.backfills.is_empty() && next.downfills.is_empty() && !super::backfills::has_evidence(tx)? {retire_sources(tx,next,previous,now)?;}
    let have_dormant: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows)",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?;
    let type_change = previous.is_some_and(|old| {
        next.tables.iter().any(|(name, t)| {
            old.table(name).is_some_and(|p| {
                t.columns
                    .iter()
                    .any(|(c, k)| p.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|old| old != k))
                    || t.constraints
                        .iter()
                        .filter(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { .. }))
                        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                        != p.constraints
                            .iter()
                            .filter(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { .. }))
                            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            })
        })
    });
    if !have_dormant && !type_change && !restored_images && tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()!=Some("true") {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let mut newly_retained = 0u64;
    // Stream on the primary key. Do not retain a store-sized list of images.
    restore_images(tx,next,previous,now,source_definitions,authority_sources,false)?;
    tx.clear_activation_scan();
    // References to a hidden row hide their dependents, including memberships.
    loop {
        let mut removed = 0u64;
        for (name, table) in next
            .tables
            .iter()
            .filter(|(n, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(n).is_none())
        {
            let t = tx.num(name)?;
            super::activation::walk(tx, t, |id, mut row| {
                if let Some(source)=authority_sources.get(name) {
                    if crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::retires_image(table,source,&row,now) {
                        if previous.and_then(|s|s.table(name)).is_none() {
                            // Whole-table source images are restored before
                            // their first index heap is installed.
                            let affected=tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
                            crate::rows::require_one(affected as u64,"row delete",name,None)?;
                            super::dormant::delete(tx,t,id,name)?;
                        } else {
                            tx.delete(name,source,&row)?;
                        }
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                }
                let native=super::backfills::native_absences(tx,t,id,&row)?;
                let source=super::backfills::native_source(previous.and_then(|s|s.table(name)).unwrap_or(table),table,&native);
                crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,source.as_ref());
                let mut causes=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
                for (column,kind) in &table.columns {
                    if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) {continue;}
                    if let Ok(value)=row.field(column) {if incompatible_column(tx,table,name,column,kind,value)? {
                        let value=match value {Value::Null=>"null".to_owned(),Value::Array(v)=>format!("a list of {} values",v.len()),_=>format!("a value outside {kind}")};
                        let remedy=if migratable(kind) {"declare a truthful scalar down projection or use a loosened bridge"} else {"computed down cannot project files, images, collections or references; use a loosened bridge"};
                        causes.insert(format!("{name}.{column} holds {value}, which does not fit {kind}; {remedy}"));
                    }}
                }
                let mut hidden=!causes.is_empty();
                for (column, kind) in &table.columns {
                    let mut edges = Vec::new();
                    if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
                        refs(kind, value, &mut edges);
                    }
                    for (parent, parent_id) in edges {
                        let missing = next.table(&parent).is_none()
                            || (!tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![tx.num(&parent)?,parent_id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
                                && reserved(tx, tx.num(&parent)?, &parent_id)?);
                        if missing {
                            causes.insert(format!("{name}.{column} references hidden parent {parent}; preserve the parent's domain in a loosened bridge"));
                            hidden = true;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if !hidden {
                    return Ok(());
                }
                super::backfills::preserve_native_absence(&mut row,&native);
                let reason=canonical_json(&causes);
                let retained: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
                    "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
                    params![t, id],
                    |r| r.get(0),
                )?;
                let mut outputs:std::collections::BTreeSet<_>=next.backfills.iter().filter(|f|f.table==*name).flat_map(|f|f.outputs.iter().cloned()).collect();
                outputs.extend(native.iter().cloned());
                if retained && !outputs.is_empty() {
                    // Preserve the original source/provenance, updating only
                    // authoritative cells this release explicitly transformed.
                    let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;
                    let mut archived:Value=serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
                    if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut archived {for c in &outputs {if let Ok(v)=row.field(c){fields.insert(c.clone(),v.clone());}else if native.contains(c){fields.remove(c);}}}
                    let definition:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;
                    let original:Table=serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
                    let definition=super::backfills::native_source(&original,table,&native);
                    tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_narrow_rows SET data=?1,definition=?4 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&archived),t,id,canonical_json(definition.as_ref())])?;
                    super::movement::rewrite_relation(tx,"_dormant_narrow_rows",t,id,&archived)?;
                }
                if retained {tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_narrow_rows SET reason=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id,reason])?;}
                for c in &outputs {
                    // Candidate-typed new references are absent from the old
                    // definition. Never erase an already-invalid binding.
                    let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,c],|r|r.get(0))?;
                    if !invalid {if let (Some(kind),Ok(value))=(table.columns.get(c),row.field(c)) {super::dormant::capture_refs(tx,t,id,c,kind,value)?;}}
                }
                if !retained {
                    newly_retained += 1;
                    let original = authority_sources.get(name).unwrap_or(table);
                    let definition=super::backfills::native_source(original,table,&native);
                    let old=definition.as_ref();
                    tx.conn().execute(
                        "INSERT INTO _dormant_narrow_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",
                        params![
                            t,
                            id,
                            canonical_json(&row),
                            canonical_json(old),
                            reason
                        ],
                    )?;
                    for (column, kind) in &old.columns {
                        // Retained cells/whole rows already captured a lifetime; moving them must not erase deletion evidence.
                        let captured:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0))?;

pub(crate) fn forward_sources(tx:&dyn Rows,next:&Schema,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    for (name,old) in sources {
        if old.through.as_ref().is_none_or(|t|t.live.is_none()) && next.table(name).and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|t|t.live.is_some()) {
            let parent=next.table(&next.tables[name].through.as_ref().unwrap().parent);
            rows(tx,name,old,parent_bytes(parent,next.tables[name].through.as_ref().and_then(|e|sources.get(&e.parent))),true,None,|row| {if tx.restored_narrowed(name,row.field("id")?.as_str()?)? {return Ok(());} forward_image(tx,next,sources,name,old,&row,now)})?;
        }
    }Ok(())
}
/// Narrowed images retain their own source definition. Admit every current
/// parent image before either forward correspondence or reverse recapture.
pub(crate) fn restored_parent_scratch(next:&Schema,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,name:&str,old:&Table)->u64 {
    let destination=next.table(name).and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref());
    let source=old.through.as_ref().filter(|e|e.live.is_some());
    [destination,source].into_iter().flatten().map(|edge| {
        parent_bytes(sources.get(&edge.parent),next.table(&edge.parent))
    }).fold(0_u64,u64::saturating_add)
}
/// Only authority cells are replaceable before narrowed restoration. Other
/// incompatible data must keep its dormant lifetime without fetching a parent.
pub(crate) fn manual_authority_fields(next:&Schema,name:&str,old:&Table)->Vec<String> {
    let Some(edge)=old.through.as_ref().filter(|e|e.live.is_some()) else {return Vec::new()};
    let Some(table)=next.table(name).filter(|t|t.through.is_none()) else {return Vec::new()};
    let Some(parent)=next.table(&edge.parent) else {return Vec::new()};
    live_correspondence(&table.rules.read,&parent.rules.read).map(|map|map.into_keys().collect()).unwrap_or_default()
}
/// Reconcile per-row provenance as well as complete-table source definitions.
pub(crate) fn manual_image(tx:&dyn Rows,next:&Schema,name:&str,old:&Table,row:&mut Value,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    let Some(edge)=old.through.as_ref().filter(|e|e.live.is_some()) else {return Ok(())};
    let Some(destination)=next.table(name).filter(|t|t.through.is_none()) else {return Ok(())};
    let parent=next.table(&edge.parent).ok_or_else(||refusal(name,1,"manual restoration needs current parent",&[]))?;
    let map=live_correspondence(&destination.rules.read,&parent.rules.read).ok_or_else(||refusal(name,1,"manual restoration needs whole current parent correspondence",&[]))?;
    if destination.columns.get(&edge.reference)!=old.columns.get(&edge.reference) || !destination.immutable_fields().any(|f|f==edge.reference) {return Err(refusal(name,1,"manual restoration must preserve immutable parent binding",&[]));}
    let p=tx.get(&edge.parent,row.field(&edge.reference)?.as_str()?)?.filter(|p|parent.live(p,now)).ok_or_else(||refusal(name,1,"manual restoration needs live current parent",&[]))?;
    let p=crate::schema::row_image(&p,parent);
    for (field,source) in map {
        if destination.engine_field(&field) || !destination.columns.contains_key(&field) {return Err(refusal(name,1,"manual authority needs ordinary destination cells",&[]));}
        let value=p.field(&source)?.clone();
        if field=="id" || field==edge.reference {if row.field(&field)?!=&value {return Err(refusal(name,1,"manual restoration cannot change identity or immutable parent binding; keep the reference and use a separate audience copy column",&[]));}}
        else if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=row {fields.insert(field,value);}
    }Ok(())
}
#[cfg(feature="sqlite")]
pub fn restore_server_images(tx:&dyn Rows,schema:&Schema,rows:impl IntoIterator<Item=(String,Value)>,now:i64)->Result<u64,RuntimeError> {
    // Same trusted final-image contract as native SQLite/Postgres. Derived
    // images are durable import input, rather than an unbounded global rebuild.
    super::live_bulk::restore(tx,schema,rows,0,now).map(|r|r.0)
}

/// Include recovered dormant source definitions, not just the immediate predecessor.
pub(crate) fn reconcile_manual(tx:&dyn Rows,next:&Schema,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.014.000#7-adoption-and-rollback — dormant manual cells are never authority after a live transfer.
    for (name,old) in sources {
        let Some(edge)=old.through.as_ref().filter(|t|t.live.is_some()) else {continue};
        let Some(destination)=next.table(name).filter(|t|t.through.is_none()) else {continue};
        let parent=next.table(&edge.parent).ok_or_else(||refusal(name,0,"reverse adoption needs its current parent",&[]))?;
        let map=live_correspondence(&destination.rules.read,&parent.rules.read)
            .ok_or_else(||refusal(name,0,"manual destination needs full current-parent audience correspondence; use a bridge release",&[]))?;
        if destination.columns.get(&edge.reference)!=old.columns.get(&edge.reference) || !destination.immutable_fields().any(|f|f==edge.reference) {
            return Err(refusal(name,0,"manual destination must preserve the immutable parent binding; use a bridge release",&[]));
        }
        let mut invalid=0;let mut samples=Vec::new();
        rows(tx,name,old,parent_bytes(Some(parent),sources.get(&edge.parent)),true,Some(destination),|stored| {
            if tx.restored_narrowed(name,stored.field("id")?.as_str()?)? {return Ok(());}
            let p=stored.field(&edge.reference).ok().and_then(|v|v.as_str().ok()).map(|id|tx.get(&edge.parent,id)).transpose()?.flatten();
            let Some(p)=p.filter(|p|parent.live(p,now)) else {invalid+=1;sample(&mut samples,&stored,&edge.reference,&edge.parent);return Ok(())};
            let p=crate::schema::row_image(&p,parent);
            let mut row=stored.clone();
            for (field,source) in &map {
                if destination.engine_field(field) || !destination.columns.contains_key(field) {return Err(refusal(name,0,"manual authority must use writable ordinary destination cells",&[]));}
                let value=p.field(source)?.clone();
                if field=="id" || field==&edge.reference {
                    if row.field(field)? != &value {return Err(refusal(name,0,"manual audience correspondence cannot change identity or immutable parent binding; keep the reference and use a separate audience copy column",&[]));}
                } else if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {fields.insert(field.clone(),value);}
            }
            tx.put(name,old,&row,Some(&stored))?;Ok(())
        })?;
        if invalid>0 {return Err(refusal(name,invalid,"manual destination needs live current parents",&samples));}
    }
    Ok(())
}

                .transpose()?

            let matches = found.as_ref().is_some_and(|p| {
                (!parent.expired(p, now) || table.expired(&row, now)) && {

    pub(crate) fn cached_authority_snapshot(&mut self,digest:&str,facts:crate::identity::facts::SessionFacts)->Result<ReadSnapshot<'_>,RuntimeError> {

    #[cfg(feature="postgres")]

            let (tx,facts)=handle.store.get_mut().planned_authority_transaction(digest,Some(crate::engine::postgres::read_plan::Plan::Validation))?;

        }
        Ok(None)
    }

    pub(crate) fn program_transaction_until(&mut self, digest: Option<&str>, principal: &str, id: Option<&str>, deadline: std::time::Instant) -> Result<AnyTx<'_>, RuntimeError> {
        #[cfg(not(feature="postgres"))]
        let _=(digest,principal,id);
        match self {
            Self::Sqlite(SqliteStore(store)) => store.program_transaction_until(deadline).map(|tx|AnyTx::Sqlite(SqliteTx(tx))),
            #[cfg(feature="postgres")]
            Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) => {
                let store=handle.store.get_mut(); store.next_program_deadline=Some(deadline);
                match digest { Some(digest)=>store.request_transaction(digest,principal,id), None=>store.transaction(Some(crate::engine::postgres::WriteMode::Serialized)) }.map(|tx|AnyTx::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx)))
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn buffered_program_transaction(&mut self, deadline: std::time::Instant) -> Result<AnyTx<'_>, RuntimeError> {
        match self {
            Self::Sqlite(SqliteStore(store)) => store.program_transaction_until(deadline).map(|tx| AnyTx::Sqlite(SqliteTx(tx))),
            #[cfg(feature="postgres")]
            Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) => {
                let store=handle.store.get_mut(); store.next_program_deadline=Some(deadline);

            },
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn planned_request_read_transaction(&mut self, digest: &str, schema: &Schema, program: Option<&Program>, context: &crate::engine::Context) -> Result<Option<ReadSnapshot<'_>>, RuntimeError> {
        #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
        if let Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) = self {
            if digest.contains('\0') { return Ok(None); }
            let plan = program.and_then(|p| crate::engine::postgres::read_plan::Plan::new(schema,p,context));
            let (tx,facts) = handle.store.get_mut().planned_authority_transaction(digest,plan)?;
            return Ok(Some(ReadSnapshot { tx: AnyTx::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx)), authority:Some((digest.into(),facts)) }));
        }
        let _ = (schema,program,context);
        self.request_read_transaction(digest)
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000.001#33-borrow-the-snapshot-never-open-another — the facts SELECT pins and validates the query's generation, identity and head.
    pub(crate) fn request_read_transaction(&mut self, digest: &str) -> Result<Option<ReadSnapshot<'_>>, RuntimeError> {
        if digest.contains('\0') { return Ok(None); }
        let (tx, facts) = match self {
            Self::Sqlite(SqliteStore(store)) => {
                let (tx, facts) = store.session_authority_transaction(digest, true)?;
                (AnyTx::Sqlite(SqliteTx(tx)), facts)
            }
            #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
            Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) => {
                let (tx, facts) = handle.store.get_mut().session_authority_transaction(digest)?;
                (AnyTx::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx)), facts)
            }
        };
        Ok(Some(ReadSnapshot { tx, authority: Some((digest.into(), facts)) }))
    }
    pub(crate) fn opening_transaction(&mut self) -> Result<AnyTx<'_>, RuntimeError> {
        match self { Self::Sqlite(SqliteStore(store)) => store.opening_transaction().map(|inner| AnyTx::Sqlite(SqliteTx(inner))), #[cfg(feature = "postgres")] Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) => { let store = handle.store.get_mut(); let mode = if store.read_only { None } else { Some(crate::engine::postgres::WriteMode::Serialized) }; store.attach_transaction(mode).map(|inner| AnyTx::Postgres(PostgresTx(inner))) } }
    }
    pub(crate) fn finish_open(tx: AnyTx<'_>) -> Result<OpeningMetadata, RuntimeError> {
        match tx { AnyTx::Sqlite(SqliteTx(tx)) => sqlite::Store::finish_open(tx), #[cfg(feature = "postgres")] AnyTx::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx)) => tx.finish_open() }
    }
    pub(crate) fn with_session_authority_snapshot<T>(&self, digest: &str, run: impl FnOnce(&AnyTx<'_>, crate::identity::facts::SessionFacts) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
        match self {
            Self::Sqlite(SqliteStore(store)) => {
                let (tx, facts) = store.session_authority_transaction(digest, true)?;
                let tx = AnyTx::Sqlite(SqliteTx(tx));
                let result = run(&tx, facts)?;
                tx.rollback()?;
                Ok(result)
            }
            #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
            Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) => {
                let mut store = handle.borrow()?;
                let (tx, facts) = store.session_authority_transaction(digest)?;
                let tx = AnyTx::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx));
                let result = run(&tx, facts)?;
                tx.rollback()?;
                Ok(result)
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn with_preflight_snapshot<T>(&self, digest: &str, receipt: Option<(&str,&str)>, ordinary: bool, run: impl FnOnce(&AnyTx<'_>,crate::identity::facts::SessionFacts)->Result<T,RuntimeError>) -> Result<T,RuntimeError> {
        #[cfg(feature="postgres")]
        if ordinary && let Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) = self {
            let mut store = handle.borrow()?;
            let (tx,facts) = store.preflight_transaction(digest,receipt)?;
            let tx = AnyTx::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx));
            let result = run(&tx,facts)?;
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(result);
        }
        let _ = (receipt,ordinary);
        self.with_session_authority_snapshot(digest,run)
    }
    pub(crate) fn with_authority_snapshot<T>(&self, run: impl FnOnce(&AnyTx<'_>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
        match self { Self::Sqlite(SqliteStore(store)) => {
            let tx = AnyTx::Sqlite(SqliteTx(store.authority_transaction()?));
            let result = run(&tx)?;
            tx.rollback()?;
            Ok(result)
        }, #[cfg(feature = "postgres")] Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) => {
            let mut store = handle.borrow()?;
            let tx = AnyTx::Postgres(PostgresTx(store.transaction(None)?));
            let result = run(&tx)?;
            tx.rollback()?;
            Ok(result)
        } }
    }
    pub fn reader(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        match self { Self::Sqlite(SqliteStore(store)) => store.reader().map(|inner| Self::Sqlite(SqliteStore(inner))), #[cfg(feature = "postgres")] Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) => handle.reader().map(|inner| Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(inner))) }
    }

    }

impl AnyStore {
    pub(crate) fn activate_at_with_launch<R>(
        &mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>,
        reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool,
        check: impl Fn(&AnyPreparation<'_, '_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>,
        build: impl FnMut(&AnyTx<'_>, bool, &mut AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>,
        finish: impl Fn(&AnyTx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<Committed<(u64, R)>, RuntimeError> {
        match self { Self::Sqlite(SqliteStore(store)) => store.activate_at_with_launch(
            schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch,
            |preparation| check(&AnyPreparation::from(preparation)),
            build, finish,
        ), #[cfg(feature = "postgres")] Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) => handle.activate(schema, programs, reset, now, check, build, finish) }
    }
}

    pub(crate) fn admit(&self, _: &Schema, _: &BTreeMap<String, Program>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> { Ok(()) }
}

impl<'store> AnyTx<'store> {
    pub(crate) fn bound_program_attempt(&self, deadline: std::time::Instant) -> Result<AttemptBudget<'_>, RuntimeError> {
        let _remaining = deadline.checked_duration_since(std::time::Instant::now()).filter(|r|!r.is_zero())
            .ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("program invocation's remaining deadline elapsed".into()))?;
        match self {
            Self::Sqlite(SqliteTx(tx)) => {
                let busy=sqlite::program_deadline::Budget::begin(tx.conn(),deadline)?;
                tx.conn().progress_handler(1000, Some(move || std::time::Instant::now() >= deadline))?;
                Ok(AttemptBudget(Some(Box::new(move || { let _ = tx.conn().progress_handler(0,None::<fn()->bool>); drop(busy); }))))
            }
            #[cfg(feature="postgres")]
            Self::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx)) => { tx.bound_program_attempt(deadline)?; Ok(AttemptBudget(None)) }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn enable_group_tail(&self) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        match self { Self::Sqlite(_) => Ok(()), #[cfg(feature="postgres")] Self::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx)) => tx.enable_group_tail() }
    }
    pub(crate) fn prepare_program_commit(&self) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        match self {
            Self::Sqlite(_) => Ok::<(), RuntimeError>(()),
            #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
            Self::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx)) => tx.flush_tail(),
        }?;
        #[cfg(test)]
        crate::engine::program_tail_prepared();
        Ok(())
    }
    pub(crate) fn commit_program(self) -> Result<(), crate::engine::committer::outcome::Failure> {
        #[cfg(any(test,feature="test-support"))]
        let started=std::time::Instant::now();
        let result=match self {
            Self::Sqlite(SqliteTx(tx)) => tx.commit_program(),
            #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
            Self::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx)) => tx.commit_program(),
        };
        #[cfg(any(test,feature="test-support"))]
        crate::round_trips::committed_in(started.elapsed());
        result
    }
    pub(crate) fn from_sqlite(tx: sqlite::Tx<'store>) -> Self { Self::Sqlite(SqliteTx(tx)) }
    // Adapter-only activation callbacks keep backend borrows scoped to the port.
    pub(crate) fn with_sqlite<T>(&self, run: impl FnOnce(&sqlite::Tx<'store>) -> T) -> T {
        match self { Self::Sqlite(SqliteTx(tx)) => run(tx), #[cfg(feature = "postgres")] Self::Postgres(_) => unreachable!("SQLite adapter transaction") }
    }
    #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
    pub(crate) fn from_postgres(tx: crate::engine::postgres::store::Tx<'store>) -> Self { Self::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx)) }
    #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
    pub(crate) fn with_postgres<T>(&self, run: impl FnOnce(&crate::engine::postgres::store::Tx<'store>) -> T) -> T {
        match self { Self::Postgres(PostgresTx(tx)) => run(tx), Self::Sqlite(_) => unreachable!("Postgres adapter transaction") }
    }
}

pub(crate) struct AttemptBudget<'a>(Option<Box<dyn FnOnce() + 'a>>);
impl Drop for AttemptBudget<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) { if let Some(clear) = self.0.take() { clear(); } }
}
#[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
impl AnyStore {
    pub(crate) fn from_postgres(handle: crate::engine::postgres::Handle) -> Self { Self::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000.000#3.7 — the committer alone owns identity COMMIT; the port preserves the original opener.
pub(crate) struct IdentitySource<'a>(pub &'a AnyStore, pub bool);
impl crate::engine::committer::TransactionSource for IdentitySource<'_> {
    fn with_transaction<T>(self, run: impl FnOnce(AnyTx<'_>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
        match self.0 {
            AnyStore::Sqlite(SqliteStore(store)) => run(AnyTx::from_sqlite(store.identity_member_transaction(self.1)?)),
            #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
            AnyStore::Postgres(PostgresStore(handle)) => {
                let mut store = handle.borrow()?;
                run(AnyTx::Postgres(PostgresTx(store.transaction(Some(crate::engine::postgres::WriteMode::Serialized))?)))
            }
        }
    }
}

   253	                    )));

   304	                    params![pt, id, source],

//! The generation barrier checks predecessor audiences before destructive projection.
// @ref LLP 3000.014#7-activation — pinned time, child scan, capture before validation, atomic publication.
use crate::{
    error::RuntimeError,
    rows::{Bounds, Dir, Rows},
    schema::{RuleExpr, Schema, Table, through::PARENT_DEADLINE},
    value::Value,
};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

const EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR: &str = "$expiry-table";

/// Whether a bare read-through changed only because its parent's read rule
/// changed.  The compiler's through identity includes that predicate, so raw
/// identity equality is deliberately too strong here; every field, lifetime,
/// type, default and provenance-bearing table fact must otherwise be equal.
pub(crate) fn predicate_only_capture_change(previous:&Schema,next:&Schema,name:&str)->bool {
    let (Some(mut old_child),Some(new_child))=(previous.table(name).cloned(),next.table(name).cloned()) else{return false};
    let (Some(old_edge),Some(new_edge))=(old_child.through.as_ref(),new_child.through.as_ref()) else{return false};
    if old_edge==new_edge || old_edge.live.is_some() || new_edge.live.is_some() || old_edge.parent!=new_edge.parent {return false}
    let (Some(mut old_parent),Some(new_parent))=(previous.table(&old_edge.parent).cloned(),next.table(&new_edge.parent).cloned()) else{return false};
    old_parent.rules.read=new_parent.rules.read.clone();
    old_child.rules.read=new_child.rules.read.clone();
    if let (Some(old),Some(new))=(old_child.through.as_mut(),new_child.through.as_ref()) {old.identity=new.identity.clone();}
    old_child==new_child && old_parent==new_parent
}

/// Rotate a sweep's table order after the last table that consumed a bounded
/// visit. Per-table row cursors alone can starve every later table when an
/// earlier table contains a permanently restricted due row.
pub(crate) fn resume_expiry_tables(
    mut tables: Vec<String>,
    cursors: &BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>,
) -> Vec<String> {
    let after = cursors
        .get(EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR)
        .and_then(|cursor| cursor.first())
        .and_then(|value| value.as_str().ok());
    if let Some(index) = after.and_then(|after| tables.iter().position(|table| table == after)) {
        let next = (index + 1) % tables.len().max(1);
        tables.rotate_left(next);
    }
    tables
}

pub(crate) fn mark_expiry_table(cursors: &mut BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>, table: &str) {
    cursors.insert(EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR.into(), vec![Value::String(table.into())]);
}

/// Only inherited batches need staging. Preserve the streaming path for
/// existing schemas, including large fixture imports.
pub(crate) fn ancestor_order<'a, I>(
    schema: &Schema,
    rows: I,
) -> Box<dyn Iterator<Item = (String, Value)> + 'a>
where
    I: IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>,
    I::IntoIter: 'a,
{
    if schema.tables.values().all(|t| t.through.is_none()) {
        return Box::new(rows.into_iter());
    }
    let order = schema.through_order();
    let mut rows: Vec<_> = rows.into_iter().collect();
    rows.sort_by_key(|(name, _)| order.iter().position(|n| n == name).unwrap_or(usize::MAX));
    Box::new(rows.into_iter())
}

fn refusal(name: &str, count: usize, reason: &str, samples: &[String]) -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural {
        code: "E_READ_THROUGH_ADOPT",
        message: format!(
            "{name}: {count} child rows cannot adopt read through: {reason}; sample ids/parent values [{}]; rewrite: reconcile the named audience fields and live parents in a bridge release, then `snapback4 deploy <dir> --restore-dormant` (guide rules)",
            samples.join(", ")
        ),
    }
}

fn sample(samples: &mut Vec<String>, row: &Value, reference: &str, parent: &str) {
    fn bounded(text: &str) -> &str {
        let mut end = text.len().min(256);
        while !text.is_char_boundary(end) {
            end -= 1;
        }
        &text[..end]
    }
    if samples.len() < 5 {
        let id = row
            .field("id")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok())
            .unwrap_or("<missing>");
        let value = row
            .field(reference)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok())
            .unwrap_or("<missing>");
        samples.push(format!(
            "{}: {reference} -> {parent}[{}]",
            bounded(id),
            bounded(value)
        ));
    }
}

fn live_work(tx:&dyn Rows,name:&str,old:Option<&Table>,next:&Schema)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    Ok(old.and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|t|t.live.is_some())
        || next.table(name).and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|t|t.live.is_some())
        || tx.has_restored_live_authority(name)?)
}

// Both definitions may coexist in a raw source image until projection.
fn parent_bytes(a:Option<&Table>,b:Option<&Table>)->u64 {
    a.into_iter().chain(b).map(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation).fold(0_u64,u64::saturating_add).saturating_mul(4)
}

fn rows(
    tx: &dyn Rows, name: &str, table: &Table, parent_scratch: u64, bounded: bool, destination: Option<&Table>,
    mut visit: impl FnMut(Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if !bounded {
        let mut bounds=Bounds::default();
        loop {
            let page=tx.scan(name,"byId",&[],&bounds,Dir::Asc,256)?;
            if page.is_empty() {return Ok(());}
            for (row,key) in page {bounds.after=crate::key::decode(&key);visit(row)?;}
        }
    }
    let decode=crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table);
    let keys=crate::rows::adoption::index_scratch(name,table).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,|t|crate::rows::adoption::index_scratch(name,t)));
    let entries=crate::rows::adoption::index_capacity(table).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,crate::rows::adoption::index_capacity)).saturating_mul(2);
    let scratch=decode.saturating_mul(4).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation).saturating_mul(4)).saturating_add(keys.saturating_mul(2))
        .saturating_add(parent_scratch).saturating_add(8192);
    tx.walk_adoption(name,decode,scratch,[2,table.columns.len() as u64+16,entries],&mut visit)
}

pub(crate) fn correspondence(
    child: &RuleExpr,
    parent: &RuleExpr,
) -> Option<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>> {
    if child.is_uniformly_readable() && parent.is_uniformly_readable() {
        return Some(Default::default());
    }
    fn walk(
        a: &serde_json::Value,
        b: &serde_json::Value,
        map: &mut std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>,

        use serde_json::Value::*;
        match (a, b) {
            (Object(a), Object(b)) if a.len() == b.len() => {
                if let (Some(String(a)), Some(String(b))) = (a.get("Field"), b.get("Field")) {
                    if map.get(a).is_some_and(|old| old != b)
                        || map.iter().any(|(old, v)| v == b && old != a)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }
                    map.insert(a.clone(), b.clone());
                    true
                } else {

//! Panel-decision witnesses on the actual activation transaction.
use super::*;

fn arm(pg: bool, relation: &str, corrupt: bool) {
    if pg {
        #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
        crate::engine::postgres::movement::fault::arm(relation, corrupt);
    } else {
        super::super::movement::fault::arm(relation, corrupt);
    }
}
fn mismatch<T>(result: Result<T, RuntimeError>) {
    let error = result
        .err()
        .expect("unverified destination must refuse before source destruction");
    assert!(
        error
            .to_string()
            .contains("SNAPBACK_ACTIVATION_COPY_MISMATCH"),
        "{error}"
    );
}
fn activate(e: &mut Engine, source: &str, n: u64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    let (s, p) = bound(source);
    let mut release = crate::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(n, Some(n - 1));
    release.restore_dormant = true;
    e.adopt_release_at(s, p, &release, false, 1000)
}
fn count(e: &mut Engine, pg: bool, relation: &str) -> i64 {
    let tx = e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();
    let n = if pg {
        #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
        {
            let sql = match relation {
                "r" => "SELECT count(*) FROM r",
                "_dormant_rows" => "SELECT count(*) FROM _dormant_rows",
                "_dormant_values" => "SELECT count(*) FROM _dormant_values",
                _ => panic!(),
            };
            tx.test_postgres_tx().query(sql, &[]).unwrap()[0].get(0)
        }
        #[cfg(not(feature = "postgres"))]
        panic!()
    } else {
        tx.test_support_conn()
            .query_row(&format!("SELECT count(*) FROM {relation}"), [], |r| {
                r.get(0)
            })
            .unwrap()
    };
    tx.rollback().unwrap();
    n
}
fn copies(pg: bool, corrupt: bool) {
    let source = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  public\n  online only\n";
    let (s, p) = bound(source);
    let (st, _, _) = store(pg);
    let mut e = Engine::new_release(st, s, p, 1, None).unwrap();
    e.seed_at(
        [(
            "notes".into(),
            object([("id", Value::Id("a".into())), ("n", Value::Int(7))]),
        )],
        1000,
    )
    .unwrap();
    let generation = e.generation();
    arm(pg, "_dormant_rows", corrupt);
    mismatch(activate(&mut e, "", 2));
    assert_eq!(e.generation(), generation);
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, pg, "r"), 1);
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, pg, "_dormant_rows"), 0);
    activate(&mut e, "", 2).unwrap();
    let generation = e.generation();
    arm(pg, "r", corrupt);
    mismatch(activate(&mut e, source, 3));
    assert_eq!(e.generation(), generation);
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, pg, "r"), 0);
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, pg, "_dormant_rows"), 1);
    activate(&mut e, source, 3).unwrap();
    let no_column = "table notes:\n  public\n  online only\n";
    arm(pg, "_dormant_values", corrupt);
    mismatch(activate(&mut e, no_column, 4));
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, pg, "r"), 1);
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, pg, "_dormant_values"), 0);
    assert_eq!(
        e.store().rows("notes").unwrap()[0].field("n").unwrap(),
        &Value::Int(7)
    );
    activate(&mut e, no_column, 4).unwrap();
    arm(pg, "r", corrupt);
    mismatch(activate(&mut e, source, 5));
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, pg, "r"), 1);
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, pg, "_dormant_values"), 1);
    activate(&mut e, source, 5).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        e.store().rows("notes").unwrap()[0].field("n").unwrap(),
        &Value::Int(7)
    );
}
#[test]
fn activation_verify_before_destroy_sqlite() {
    for corrupt in [false, true] {
        copies(false, corrupt)
    }
}
#[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
#[test]
#[ignore = "private PG16; verify exact destination before destruction"]
fn activation_verify_before_destroy_postgres() {
    for corrupt in [false, true] {
        copies(true, corrupt)
    }
}

#[test]
#[ignore = "private PG16; captured OIDs survive late activation DDL"]
fn activation_replaced_heap_oid_refuses() {
    let (s, p) = bound("table notes:\n  n: int\n  public\n  online only\n");
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, pg, "r"), 1);
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, pg, "_dormant_values"), 1);
    activate(&mut e, source, 3).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        e.store().rows("notes").unwrap()[0].field("n").unwrap(),
        &Value::Int(7)
    );
}

#[test]
fn dormant_asset_rename_reorders_keys_before_id_sqlite() {
    for (old, new) in [("photo", "album"), ("zebra", "apple")] {
        let source = format!(
            "table photos:\n  {old}: file <=1000\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(blob: file <=1000):\n  return insert photos {{{old}:blob}}\n"
        );
        let (s, p) = bound(&source);
        let (st, _, _) = store(false);
        let mut e = Engine::new_release(st, s, p, 1, None).unwrap();
        let asset = e
            .store_file(
                "alice",
                b"legal rename",
                Some("text/plain"),
                "image.txt",
                100,
            )
            .unwrap();
        let handle =
            Value::Map(BTreeMap::from([("id".into(), Value::Id(asset.id.clone()))]).into());
        let out = e
            .mutate(
                "add",
                None,
                crate::Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("blob".into(), handle)])).at(101),
            )
            .unwrap();
        let crate::engine::WriteState::Sent { result, .. } = out.state else {
            panic!("{out:?}")
        };
        let id = result.field("id").unwrap().as_str().unwrap().to_owned();
        activate(&mut e, "", 2).unwrap();
        let renamed = source
            .replace(
                &format!("{old}: file <=1000"),
                &format!("{new}: file <=1000 renamed from {old}"),
            )
            .replace(&format!("{{{old}:blob}}"), &format!("{{{new}:blob}}"));
        activate(&mut e, &renamed, 3).unwrap();
        let row = e.store().rows("photos").unwrap().pop().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            crate::assets::asset_id(row.field(new).ok()),
            Some(asset.id.as_str())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            e.store().asset(&asset.id).unwrap().unwrap().home,
            Some(("photos".into(), id, new.into()))
        );
        assert!(row.field(old).is_err());
    }
}

#[test]
fn restored_cell_legal_backfill_changes_expected_final_image_sqlite() {
    let source = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
    let (s, p) = bound(source);
    let (st, _, _) = store(false);
    let mut e = Engine::new_release(st, s, p, 1, None).unwrap();
    e.seed_at(
        [(
            "notes".into(),
            object([("id", Value::Id("a".into())), ("n", Value::Int(7))]),
        )],
        1000,
    )
    .unwrap();
    activate(&mut e, &source.replace("  n: int\n", ""), 2).unwrap();
    let patched = format!("{source}backfill notes patch(n: int):\n  {{n:n + 1}}\n");
    activate(&mut e, &patched, 3).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        e.store().rows("notes").unwrap()[0].field("n").unwrap(),
        &Value::Int(8)
    );
    assert_eq!(count(&mut e, false, "_dormant_values"), 0);
}

fn retained_rehide_native_agreement_advances_expected_image_sqlite() {
        let count: i64 = self.tx.prepare_cached("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT 1 FROM i WHERE ti = ?1 AND k >= ?2 AND k < ?3 LIMIT ?4)")?.query_row(params![ti, base, hi, limit as i64], |row| row.get(0))?;
        Ok(count as u64)
    }

    /// Write a row image: a fresh insert when `old` is none, else a replace
    /// that touches only the index entries whose keys changed (a `words`
    /// index has one per token). Returns the number of entries written.
    pub fn put(&self, table_name: &str, table: &Table, row: &Value, old: Option<&Value>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.put_indexed(table_name, table, row, old, "INSERT INTO i(ti, k, id) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)")
    }

    /// A replacement loader has already cleared the rows and created its
    /// temporary index heap. It must install those indexes before any read.
    /// Intended caller: `snapback4-device::sqlite`.
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.4 — native replacement sorts complete indexes inside the publication transaction.
    pub fn put_loading_indexes(&self, table_name: &str, table: &Table, row: &Value) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.put_indexed(table_name, table, row, None, "INSERT INTO temp._device_index_load(ti, k, id) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)")
    }

    fn put_indexed(&self, table_name: &str, table: &Table, row: &Value, old: Option<&Value>, insert_index: &str) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.clear_activation_scan();
        if table.ephemeral.is_some() { return Err(RuntimeError::Store("durable transaction cannot write an ephemeral table".into())); }
        let t = self.num(table_name)?;
        let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?;
        narrowed::check_write(self,table_name,table,row,old)?;
        backfills::observe_write(self,t,id,table,row,old)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#204-reference-renames-and-asset-extraction — an admitted extraction child must contain a mapped asset. Asset-free tables cannot own an active extraction receipt, so ordinary writes need no metadata probe.
        if table.columns.values().any(|kind|kind.contains_asset()) {
            extractions::observe_write(self,t,id,row,old)?;
        }
        let keys = crate::rows::index_write(table_name, table, row, old, id)?;
        self.changes.borrow_mut().record(table_name, table, old, Some(row));
        let data = canonical_json(row);
        let sql = if old.is_some() { "UPDATE r SET data = ?3 WHERE t = ?1 AND id = ?2" } else { "INSERT INTO r(t, id, data) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)" };
        let affected = self.tx.prepare_cached(sql)?.execute(params![t, id, data])?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000.002#3.3 — solo recovery must reject unapplied images just like bulk apply.
        if affected != 1 {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Store(format!("row write count mismatch for {table_name}: expected 1, got {affected}")));
        }
        if self.lifecycle { movement::rewrite(self,t,id,row)?; }
        let mut insert = self.tx.prepare_cached(insert_index)?;
        let mut entries = 0;
        for crate::rows::IndexWrite { name, before, after } in keys {
            if before == after { continue; }
            let ti = self.num(&format!("{table_name}.{name}"))?;
            for key in before.iter().filter(|key| !after.contains(key)) {

Shared tables retain the **current authored authority**, including transition
bindings, read rules, payload guards and field ownership. The compiled bound IR
must equal current authority after declared identity alignment. A release switch
is a view over the same current authoritative rows, never restoration of a
historical database snapshot: deleted rows stay deleted and current values
stay current except declared truthful migrations. No resurrection or replay of
historical preimages over authored current images is authorized. With no
hiding and no domain enlarging beyond current, those row populations remain
the current populations; changing read visibility still follows exact current
authority.
For intentional hiding, IR equality alone is insufficient: §20.2 additionally
checks population-sensitive authority/guards under the actual writer. Removed old immutability/uniqueness
constraints follow explicitly relaxed current constraints. Newer-only tables
retain current rules without promising old clients expose new concepts. Native
or worker dependencies without a qualified authored bundle refuse. Undefined
fields, changed helper closures and incompatible workflows refuse rather than
weakening guards. Nullable result fields and carried/reused guards appear in
an action-focused checklist; successful mechanical adaptations are facts, not
11–42 mandatory decisions. The commissioning author's chosen visible-rollback
contract explicitly permits nullable/wider result domains; these are disclosed
as RESULT compatibility changes, never claimed to retain a nonnull v1 result
contract. Requiring nonnull results would contradict that requested end game.
Names and accepted input contracts remain preserved. Dormant restoration advice
names --restore-dormant.

Removing a defaulted predecessor column requires an authored truthful down.
The initial grammar has no acknowledged-absence escape for removed defaults.
Preview may suggest a cross-table expression from declared references/columns,
but it is advisory source for the author to review, never an inferred business
mapping. A false default is not a reversible representation.

Prepared source is immutable. Each parent manifest maps original target id to
numeric view id, executable digest, changed columns, carried guards and hiding
reason. The parent's full control digest includes the manifest; the view's full
control digest includes its metadata. Prepared views are usable only after the
parent's CAS activation commits and never count as ordinary latest or equal
forward source. Failed parents remove stages/views. Unknown admitted outcomes
retain them for history-authoritative recovery; uncommitted parents cannot
authorize selection. Before either equal-source no-op or retained-source reactivation, prepare the
complete desired view set privately and compare target/executable digest/hiding
reasons with that source's own committed manifest, verifying its entries. A
matching set permits no-op or identity reuse. A missing, stale or newly needed
mapping creates an ordinary registration parent using the same source and full
adoption/CAS validation; no view is selectable before that commit. Output and
JSON identify the equivalent retained source as sourceRelease. Source and old
manifests remain immutable. Temporary desired views are removed on no-op/reuse,
refusal and preview. This also upgrades unchanged legacy deployments without
needed registrations. Later equal source is a no-op once its desired set matches.
Retained-source reactivation and rollback preserve their own registry context.

registered parent of the active compiled view, under the command lock. Never
borrow a later deployment's view across source branches: it may carry weaker
guards than the active source. If that context has no mapping, default selection
uses original source with the same writer visibility checks. Bind actual source
digest, expected release and activation revision to the existing CAS. ABA/equal-content switches change revision. Cold, hot,
preview and restart use that actual immutable source. Selection restores dormant
